iḷaṅkumaraṉār
tamiḻvaḷam
26
1. kaḻaka amiccar tiruvaraṅkar varalāṟu
āciriyar :
mutu muṉivar irā.iḷaṅkumaraṉ

vaḷavaṉ patippakam
ceṉṉi - 600 017.


nūṟ kuṟippu
nūṟpeyar	:	iḷaṅkumaraṉār - tamiḻvaḷam 26
āciriyar	:	irā. iḷaṅkumaraṉ
patippāḷar	:	kō. iḷavaḻakaṉ
patippu	:	2013
tāḷ	:	16 ki veḷḷittāḷ
aḷavu	:	1/8 temmi
eḻuttu	:	11 puḷḷi
pakkam	:	16 + 168 = 184
nūl kaṭṭamippu	:	iyalpu (cātāraṇam)
vili	:	urupā. 115/-
paṭikaḷ	:	1000

veḷiyīṭu
vaḷavaṉ patippakam
eṇ : 2 ciṅkāra vēlar teru,
tiyākarāyar nakar, ceṉṉi - 600 017.
tolipēci : 24339030


patippuri
ilakkiya ilakkaṇac cemmal mutumuṉivar ayyā iḷaṅkumaraṉār avarkaḷ eḻupattintu āṇṭu niṟivu eytiyatip pōṟṟum vakiyil tamiḻ iḷiyarkkeṉṟu avar eḻutiya nūlkaḷiyellām cērttu eḻupattintu nūlkaḷ kaṭanta 2005 - ām āṇṭu tiruccit tirunakaril veḷiyiṭṭu em patippup paṇikkup perumi cērttuk koṇṭōm. 
ipperuntamiḻ aṟiñar eḻutiya paṭippukaḷi 20 tokutikaḷāka (1 mutal 20 vari) avariṉ 81-ām āṇṭu niṟiviyoṭṭi kaṭanta 2009 ām āṇṭil veḷiyiṭṭup perumi cērttōm. perumikkup perumi cērkkum vakiyil avar tam aṟivu viḷiccali 21 mutal 40 vari 
20 tokutikaḷi  83-ām āṇṭu (2012) niṟiviyoṭṭi tamiḻulakam payaṉkoṇṭu ceḻikkum vakiyil veḷiyiṭukiṟōm. avar tam aruntamiḻp paṇiyi vaṇaṅki makiḻkiṟōm.
ipperumakaṉ vāḻum kālattilēyē ivar eḻutiya eḻuttukkaḷ, pēciya pēccukkaḷ aṉittiyum tokuttu veḷiyiṭuvatu eṉpatu tamiḻnūl patippu varalāṟṟil pōṟṟi makiḻvataṟkāṉa,  aṉivarum piṉpaṟṟuvataṟkāṉa arumperum tamiḻp paṇiyākum.
ivar tannalam karutāmal tamiḻ nalam kāttu varupavar.  tammimuṉṉiṟuttātu  tamiḻi muṉṉiṟuttum peruntamiḻaṟiñar.  tamakkeṉa vāḻātu tamiḻkkeṉa vāḻpavar. orunāḷiṉ muḻup
poḻutum tamiḻākavē vāḻum tamiḻp pōrāḷi. caṅkac cāṉṟōr variciyil vittu eṇṇattakka aruntamiḻaṟiñar. tamiḻ ilakkaṇa - ilakkiya marapik kāttu varum marapu vaḻi aṟiñar. āravāram mikunta iṉṟiya cūḻniliyil paṭāṭōpam iṉṟiyum, viḷamparap pōlimi iṉṟiyum,   tamiḻ moḻiyiṉ āḻa akalaṅkaḷi akaḻntu kāṭṭum toltamiḻaṟiñar. moḻinūl katiravaṉ pāvāṇariṉ vērccol āyvil avar kāṭṭiya vaḻiyil tam tamiḻāyvit  toṭarpavar. 
tamiḻāyvuk kaḷattil  tam āyvuppayaṇattit toṭaṅkum tamiḻ āyvāḷarkaḷukkum, tamiḻ uṇarvāḷarkaḷukkum, māṇavac celvaṅkaḷukkum taṅkat taṭṭil vittup poṟ
kuviyalākat  tantuḷḷōm. em tamiḻ nūl patippup payaṇattil,  tamiḻtteṉṟal tiru.vi.ka.viyum, taṉittamiḻiyakkat tanti maṟimaliyaṭikaḷiyum, moḻinūl katiravaṉ pāvāṇariyum, tamiḻ moḻikkāvalar ilakkuvaṉāriyum vaḻikāṭṭiyākak koṇṭu tamiḻnūl parappiṉ elliyik kaṇṭu kāṭṭum caṅkattamiḻc cāṉṟōrāka viḷaṅkum aiyā iḷaṅkumaraṉār  vāḻum kālattilēyē avar eḻutiya nūlkaḷi veḷiyiṭuvati yām peṟṟa pēṟākak karutukiṟōm. tamiḻar illamtōṟum irukkattakka ivvaruntamiḻc celvaṅkaḷit tamiḻ iḷam talimuṟikku vippākak koṭuttuḷḷōm. 
 caluki pōṉāl pōkaṭṭum - eṉ 
	aluval pōṉāl pōkaṭṭum 
	talimuṟi orukōṭi kaṇṭa - eṉ
	tamiḻ viṭutali ākaṭṭum.
pāvēntar varikaḷi niṉivil koḷvōm.
- patippāḷar.




peṟum pēṟu
vaḷam eṅkē irukkiṟatu?
vaḷam eṅkēyum irukkiṟatu! aḵtu illāta iṭamē illi! 
vaḷam eṅkum iruppati eppaṭi aṟiyalām?
vaḷattit tēṭum uḷam iruntāl, vaḷam eṅkēyum iruppati aṟiyalām?
vaḷattiṉ aḷavu eṉṉa?
vaḷattiṉ aḷavum uḷattiṉ aḷavēyām!
uḷattil vaḷam illiyāṉāl, uḷḷa vaḷamum uṭiyavaṉukkum utavātu ūrukkum ulakukkum utavātu!
vaḷattit tarum uḷatti oruvar vāykkap peṟṟāl pōtum!
avvaḷam kuṉṟiyaḷavāṉāl eṉṉa?
     kuṉṟattaṉiya vaḷamāṉāl eṉṉa?
     tiṉi aḷavāṉāl eṉṉa?
     paṉi aḷavāṉāl eṉṉa?
     vaḷam vaḷamē!
tēṉī vaḷam? tiṉittaṉi aḷavup pūvil, tēṉī koḷḷum tēṉ evvaḷavu irukkum?
kōttumpi koṇarum tēṉ vaḷam evvaḷavu irukkum?
kōttumpi koṇarvatē vaḷam, tēṉī koṇarvatu vaḷamilli eṉa evarāvatu kūṟuvārā?
vaḷam vaḷamē! avaravar uḻippu, aṟivuttiṟam, uṇmiyaṟivu, paṭṭaṟivu, kāla-iṭa-cūḻal koṭi- ākiyavaṟṟip poṟuttatu atu.
poruḷ maṭṭumā vaḷam?
pulami vaḷamilliyā?
ūkkutal utavutal uṟutuṇiyātal oṉṟutal āyavi - vaḷamilliyā?
ellām vaḷamē! mallal vaḷaṉē eṉpatu tolkāppiyam. (uri.7) uṭalālum vaḷam; uḻippālum vaḷam; uṇarvālum vaḷam; uriyālum vaḷam; vaḷam paṭa vāḻa vēṇṭātār evar? vaḷam peṟa vēṇṭātār evar?
vaḷantali mayaṅkiya vañci muṟṟam
eṉpatu cilampu! vaḷattiṉ aḷavu, aḷantu kaṭi aṟiyāvaḷam eṉpatu (25:33-34)
nāṭeṉpa nāṭā vaḷattaṉa
eṉpatu nāṭṭiṉ ilakkaṇam! eḷimi, iṉimi, iṟimi, niṟimi eṉa makkaḷ vāḻnta nāḷ ilakkaṇam! iṉṟō,
nāṉila vaḷaṅkaḷiyum nāṭā nāṭilli!
enta nāṭu ēṟṟumati ceyyavilli?
enta nāṭu iṟakkumati ceyyavilli?
pālivaḷatti nāṭiccōli niṟṟal veḷippaṭi eṉṟāl, colvāṉēṉ?
nāṭāvaḷattil kuppikkīri cērātā? kuṟuntūṟu cērātā?
vaḷatti vaḻaṅkutal tāṉ piṟavik kaṭaṉē yaṉṟi aḷaviṭum poṟuppu vaḻaṅkuvārkku illi! peṟuvār poṟuppu. āṉāl,
vaḷam peṟṟup peṟṟup peruki vāḻntavar, avvaḷattit tamakku vaḻaṅkiya maṇṇukku maṟittu vaḻaṅkip peṟṟa kaṭaṉit tīrkkāmal pōṉāl kaṭaṉkārarākavē pōvatu maṭṭumaṉṟi, piṟavi aṭiyāḷamē illāmalum pōyviṭuvār!
peṟṟavar vaḻiyākak karuvilē peṟṟa tiru eṉṉa! kaṟṟa kalviyāl peṟṟa vaḷam eṉṉa! paṭṭaṟivāl - āḻmaṉa āyvāl - tēṭik koṇṭa vaḷaṅkaḷ eṉṉa eṉṉa! piñcup paruva mutal perum pirivu vari peṟṟavaṟṟi, annilipparuvattarkkup paruvamaḻi pōlap palappala vakiyālum vaḻaṅkutal vāḻvār vāḻvuc cīrmiyātal vēṇṭum!
purivu terinta nāḷ mutal pulami perukivarum aḷavukkut takat-takat tolkāppiyar āṇi vaḻi,
ōytal āytal niḻattal cāay
         āvayiṉ nāṉkum uḷḷataṉ nuṇukkam
eṉṉum nālneṟik kaṭaṉkaḷiyum niṟivēṟṟal vēṇṭum! kūṟṟiyum āṭal koḷḷum koḷki ituveṉa uṇarvār ōyār, oḻiyār; cāyār; carintum pōkār!
eṉ iḷantip paruvamē, āciriyap paruvamākiviṭṭatu.  akkālac cūḻal atu.
piṟantanāḷ : 30.01.1930
āciriyap paṇi ēṟṟa nāḷ: 08.04.1946
kāl caṭṭi pōṭum māṇavapparuvattil vēṭṭi kaṭṭum āciriyaṉāki viṭṭēṉ! mūṉṟāṇṭu āciriyap paṇi ceytāl pōtum! pulavar tērvit taṉiyāka eḻutalām eṉṉum vāyppu! avvāyppic cikkeṉap paṟṟiyamiyāl 1949, 1950, 1951 ākiya mūvāṇṭukaḷil toṭarntu nuḻivut tērvu, pulavar iṭinilit tērvu, pulavar niṟi nilit tērvu eṉac ciṟappuṟa veṟṟi vāyppu eytiyatu.  uyarpaḷḷi, mēlpaḷḷi eṉpavaṟṟilum palkalik kaḻakat tamiḻiyal āyvuk kaḷattilumāka ēṟattāḻa 43 āṇṭukaḷ tamiḻvāḻvāka vāḻa vāyttatu.  tamiḻttoṇṭum, tamiḻneṟit toṇṭum eṉṟum viṭutal aṟiyā viruppoṭu ceytu koṇṭiruttalē iyaṟkiyōṭu iyinta vāḻvāki viṭṭatu!
pulamit tērvukkuriya pāṭattaḷavilō, nūlkaḷiṉ aḷavilō eṉ kalvi niṉṟatu illi! tolkāppiyam mutal iṟṟināḷ nūlkaḷ vari iyaṉṟa aḷavāl tamiḻvaḷam peṟutali nōṉpākak koṇṭu kaṟkum pēṟupeṟṟēṉ.  pērāciriyar ci.ilakkuvaṉār, moḻiñāyiṟu pāvāṇar, nāvalar ca.cō.pāratiyār, varalāṟṟuc cemmal aracamāṇikkaṉār, pintamiḻp pāvalar m.ki.guªjhkdh®, urivēntar auvi cu.turicāmiyār, mūtaṟiñar cemmal va,cupa.māṇikkaṉār, tāmaric celvar va,cuppiyaṉār, īrōṭu vēlā, kuḻittali mī.cu. iḷamuruku poṟcelvi, patippuc cemmal meyyappaṉār, pārati cō.cāminātaṉār, tamiḻppōrāḷi iḷavaḻakaṉār, tamiḻ mīṭpukkut taliniṉṟa āṉārūṉā, pāvāṇar aṟakkaṭṭaḷiyar kōvalaṅkaṇṇaṉār, maliyaka māriyappaṉār iṉṉar nerukkat toṭarpukaḷ vāyttaṉa.
iḷantip paruvattilēyē pāvēntar, ōki cuttāṉanta pāratiyār, kavimaṇiyār, kavirāca paṇṭita cekavīra
pāṇṭiyaṉār, aṟiñar mu.va. āyōr ariya kāṭciyum ciṉmuṟic cantippukaḷum eṉṉuḷ pacumi nalkiṉa. 
tamiḻp poḻivukaḷum, āyvuk kaṭṭurikaḷum, kuṭumpac caṭaṅkukaḷum eṉ mīṭṭeṭuppup paṇikaḷ āyiṉa!
em kuṭumpak kaṭamikaḷ em paṇiyiyō toṇṭiyō kavarntu koḷḷā vakiyil iṉiya tuṇiyum makkaḷum amintaṉar.
ataṉāl, kālamellām kaṟkavum kaṟpikkavum nūl
paṭikkavum vāytta vāyppup peritāyiṟṟu.
aṉpum aṟivum eṉṉum nūl toṭaṅkic centamiḻc coṟporuḷ kaḷañciyam eṉat toṭarntu ēṟattāḻa 400 nūlkaḷ iyaṟṟavum veḷiyiṭavum vāyttaṉa.
kuḻanti nūlkaḷ, nūlaka nūlkaḷ, āyvu nūlkaḷ, akarātikaḷ, urinūlkaḷ, patippu nūlkaḷ, pā nūlkaḷ, kāppiyaṅkaḷ, katikaḷ, kaṭṭurikaḷ, tokuppukaḷ ilakkaṇa nūlkaḷ eṉap palappala tiṟattaṉavāyp pāl vāyp paruvattar mutal palkalik kaḻaka āyvar varikkum payaṉ koḷḷum vakiyil avi amintaṉa.
eti eṭuttālum pāṭalākkal orukālam; katiyum nāṭakamum ākkal orukālam; patippup paṇiyē oru kālam; uri
kāṇalē oru kālam; paṭippaṭiyē collāyvē vāḻveṉa ākiviṭṭa kālam eṉa amintaṉa.
ik koṭikaḷ vaḻaṅkiyavi pārati patippakam, civacittānta nūṟpatippuk kaḻakam, maṇi patippakam, murukaṉ patippakam, vēlā patippakam, maṇivācakar patippakam, vēmaṉ patippakam, mūvēntar patippakam, pāvāṇar aṟakkaṭṭaḷi, cākittiya akātemi, ulakat tamiḻ ārāycci niṟuvaṉam, tamiḻmaṇ patippakam,  amiḻtam patippakam, vaḷavaṉ patippakam, māṇavar patippakam, amiḻtamaṇi patippakam, tiruvaḷḷuvar tavaccāli aṟakkaṭṭaḷi veḷiyīṭukaḷ eṉa virintaṉa.
nūlurup peṟṟu accaka vaḻiyum patippaka vaḻiyum kāṇappeṟāmalum etirpārā maṟivukaḷālum ēṟattāḻac ciṟitum peritumāy oḻintavi irupatukku mēl āyiṉa.
centamiḻ, centamiḻc celvi, tamiḻppāvi, kuṟaḷ
neṟi, kuṟaḷiyam, malarkaḷ, āyvaraṅkaṅkaḷ eṉak kaṭṭurikaḷ perukiṉa.  accukku varātaviyum ākiṉa!  ivi eṉṟum orumukamāyk kiṭikka vāyppilli.
em nūlkaḷ emmiṭamē muppatukkumēl illāmal maṟintaṉa.  eḻutiyaṉa ellāvaṟṟiyum orumukamākap peṟap
palar avāviṉār. tavattiru āttumāṉanta aṭikaḷār (āruyirkku aṉpar) atil talippaṭṭu niṉṟār.  āyar āṇṭaki cūcimāṇikkaṉār cuṭarēṟṟiṉār. pāratitācaṉ palkalikkaḻakam matippuṟu mutumuṉivar paṭṭam vaḻaṅkiyatu.  ataṟku makiḻvuṟṟa vakiyāl pāvāṇar tamiḻiyakkam mutalām amippukaḷum, muṉivar tirumāṟaṉār mutalām ārvalarkaḷum viḻā oṉṟu eṭuttaṉar.  avviḻāt talimiyit tiruttaku tuṇivēntar poṉṉavikkō ēṟṟār.  tavattiru āttumāṉanta aṭikaḷār vāḻtturittār.  poṟiñar tamiḻaṟiñar pālakaṅkātaraṉār eṉ nūlkaḷ cilavaṟṟi oḷippaṭiyākki uyaraṭṭik kaṭṭil vaḻaṅki avaṟṟi muḻutuṟap peṟa mīḷacciṭutal vēṇṭum eṉṟār.  tamiḻākkap patippē vāḻvāka oṉṟiya iḷavaḻakaṉār muḻutuṟa acciṭa oppiṉār.  aṭikaḷār veḷiyīṭṭup poṟuppu ēṟpatākak kūṟiṉār. kiṭikkum nūlkaḷ tavirttup piṟavaṟṟiyellām acciṭum tiṭṭam koṇṭu ēṟattāḻa irupatu, irupatu tokutikaḷāka veḷiyiṭuvatu eṉa uṟutippaṭuttap paṭṭatu.  avvakiyil vāyppatē, iḷaṅkumaraṉār tamiḻvaḷam eṉṉum it toki nūlkaḷām! it tokiyil viṭupaṭum nūlkaḷ kaṭṭurikaḷ, pāṭalkaḷ palapala;  tokukka vāykkā vakiyil kiṭṭāvakiyil maṟappu vakiyil viṭupāṭuṭiyavi uḷa.  eṉiṉum toṇṇuṟu viḻukkāṭēṉum oru mukamākap peṟum pēṟu vāykkiṉṟatu eṉpatu makiḻvūṭṭuvatām!
makkaḷ vāḻvu vaḷarniliyatu! nūlkaḷum vaḷar niliyavi! ātalāl oruvāḻvuk koṭi maṭṭumaṉṟu ītu; oruvāḻvu peṟat takka palparuvak koṭiyumām eṉal cālum! itaṉ aṭippaṭiyām ettēṭalilum peritum tōyāmal tamiḻvaḷat tēṭalilēyē kālamellām cōmpalic cuṭṭerittu, iṭukkaṇukku iṭukkaṇ ākki vāḻnta vāḻvuk koṭiyām itu!
ivvaḷatti muḻutuṟa eṇṇiṉ oṉṟu uṟutiyāka veḷippaṭum.  nalavāḻvu nalvāḻvu karutāta - iyaṟki iṟimip potumi karutāta - eppaṭippum empaṭippil irā eṉpatēyām.  ivaṟṟip payilvār peṟum pēṟum atuvāka amiyiṉ appēṟē yām peṟum pēṟām.
ivvaḷam veḷivara virumpiṉōr uḻittōr utaviṉōr āya aṉivarkkum naṉṟik kaṭappāṭuṭiyēṉ! vāḻiya nalaṉē! vāḻiya nilaṉē!
nellukku umiyuṇṭu
nīrukku nuriyuṇṭu
pullitaḻ pūvukkum uṇṭu
kuṟikaḷintu niṟi peytu
kaṟṟal, kaṟpōr kaṭaṉeṉal muntiyar muṟi
iṉpa aṉpuṭaṉ,
irā.iḷaṅkumaraṉ

mukavuri
ṭākṭar pā. naṭarācaṉ
kaḻaka amiccar tiruvaraṅkar varalāṟu eṉpatu, avar vāḻnta kālattiya tamiḻmoḻi, civattiṉ varalāṟu eṉak kūṟalām. kaṟṟōrkaḷ, tamiḻil pēcutal tamakkuc ciṟappaḷikkā teṉak karutiya akkālattu ivar tāymoḻippaṟṟiṉ mikutiyāl, ammoḻiyām tamiḻukkuk kaḻakam kaṇṭu, pakalum iravum ayarātu pāṭupaṭṭup pārellām pōṟṟa, ak kaṉṉimoḻiyiṉ vaḷam perukki vaḷarcci kaṇṭavar. ilakkiyam, ilakkaṇam, camayam, varalāṟu, tiṟaṉāyvu, moḻipeyarppu, icittamiḻ vaḷarcci, nāṭakattamiḻp perukkam pōṉṟa iṉṉum palvēṟu tuṟikaḷil nūlkaḷ toṭarntuvara, tam iḷiyavar tiru.va.cuppiyāpiḷḷi avarkaḷukku vaḻi kāṭṭiyāy iruntavar tiruvaraṅkarēyāvar.
ivar neñcuram mikkavar. taḷarāta muyaṟciyum caliyāta uḻippum koṇṭavar. vāḻviṉ uyariya kuṟikkōḷkaḷit teḷivākat tērntu kāṇum iyalpiṉar; iṉiya collālum, itam mikka naṭattiyālum paḻakiyavar neñcaṅkaḷil niliyāṉa iṭampeṟṟavar. kuṭumpattiyum, cārntavariyum pēṇum kuṇanalam vāynta cemmal. tamiḻmoḻiyiṉpālum, civattiṉpālum ivarukkirunta āḻamāṉa īṭupāṭu ivari aṉivarum pōṟṟum arumaṇiyāka - cuṭarmaṇiyāka ākkiṟṟu.
ciṉṉañciṟu paruvattil tam tantiyār tiru. vayiramuttup piḷḷi avarkaḷi iḻanta tiruvaraṅkarum, ivar tam uṭaṉpiṟantōrum taṉ aṉṉiyār cuntarattammiyār aravaṇippilēyē vāḻavēṇṭiyavarkaḷāyiṉar. vaṟumi
yiṉ tākkutalāl tiruvaraṅkar kaṟṟuvanta kalvikkut taṭiyēṟ
paṭṭatu. ētēṉum paṇiceytu poruḷīṭṭa viḻinta ivar, tūttukkuṭi vantu ciṟṟāḷ vēliyil īṭupaṭṭār. aṅku ivarukku vaḻutūr aḻakiya cuntarampiḷḷi eṉpavar toṭarpu ēṟpaṭṭatu. ip periyār ciṟanta civaneṟiyāḷar, tavattiru maṟimaliyaṭikaḷār tiṟaṉ aṟinta cemmal; aṭikaḷāriṭam pēraṉpum perumatippum koṇṭavar; tūttukkuṭi civa cittānta capiyil īṭupāṭum ivarukkiruntatu. ivaratu tuṇiyum tūttukkuṭit taṅkalum tiruvaraṅkar vāḻvil perum tiruppatti ēṟpaṭuttiṉa. eṉiṉum tam vāḻvil takka poruḷvaḷam ēṟpaṭavilliyē eṉṟa kavali ivarukkiruntatu. eṉavē ivar tūttukkuṭiyil nilittirukka virumpavilli. vaḻutūr aḻakiya cuntaraṉār ālōcaṉiyiṉpaṭi koḻumpu ceṉṟār.
aṅku oru vaṇika niṟuvaṉattil kaṇakkeḻutum paṇiyil amarnta tiruvaraṅkaṉār tam uyariya paṇpukaḷālum, ūṟṟeṉac curantu poṅkum tamiḻp paṟṟu, civappaṟṟukaḷālum koḻumpu vaṇikap perumakkaḷiṭam mikka nerukkamuṭiyava
rāṉār.
tavattiru maṟimaliyaṭikaḷārik kuṟittu ēṟkaṉavē kēḷvippaṭṭirunta tiruvaraṅkar, mēlum avartam ciṟappiyalpu kaḷi aṟintu, avarit tam uḷḷattil niliniṟutti vaḻipaṭṭu vantār. aṭikaḷāri īḻattukkaḻittum payaṉpeṟa eṇṇiṉār. ivaratu viruppattukku icivaḷittaṉar aṅkuḷḷa tamiḻpperumakkaḷ,
irumuṟi tavattiru maṟimaliyaṭikaḷāri ilaṅkikku varavaḻittu, centamiḻccelvam paṟṟiyum, civaviḷakkam kuṟittum aṅkuḷḷa paṟpala ūrkaḷil coṟpōrukku nikaḻtta vittār tiruvaraṅkar aṭikaḷār iṉiya ariya urimaṇi
kaḷik kēṭkum makkaḷellām āṉantakkaṭalil mūḻkiṉar. itaṟku vāyppaḷitta tiruvaraṅkari vaṇikapperumakkaḷum ēṉiya cāṉṟōrum pārāṭṭip pukaḻntaṉar.
tiruvaraṅkar ceyta maṟṟoru arumperum ceyaliyum iṅkuk kuṟippiṭavēṇṭum. aṭikaḷāriṉ poruḷ tēviyi uṇarntu pala perumakkaḷiṭam paṇam tiraṭṭi avarukku utaviṉār ivar. itu eḷitāṉa ceyalō? yāvarālum ceyyatakkatō? ivvutavi aṭikaḷārukku mikka payaṉi aḷittatu. avar toṭaṅkiyirunta māḷikip paṇi niṟivēṟuvataṟkum, putiya accakam toṭaṅkuvataṟkum, avaratu aṟivuk kaṭal itaḻic cemmiyāy naṭattuvataṟkum payaṉpaṭṭatu. mēlum aṭikaḷāri ūkki arumperum nūlkaḷ ākkavum, āyvuppaṇikaḷic cevvaṉē ceyyavum vāyppaḷittatu. tiruvaraṅkariṉ ivvarum utaviyi aṭikaḷārē naṉṟip pēruṇarcciyil pala kaṭitaṅkaḷ eḻutip pārāṭṭiṉā reṉṟāl vēṟu eṉṉa vēṇṭum!
ilaṅkiyiliruntu tāyakam tirumpiya tiruvaraṅkar ceṉṉikku vantu, aṅkut tam tirucaṅkar kampeṉiyit toṭaṅki naṭattiyatum, tavattiru maṟimaliyaṭikaḷār tamiḻp paṇi vaḷarcciyik karuttil koṇṭēyākum. ceṉṉiyil iruppiṉ aṭikaḷārōṭum neruṅkip paḻakavum avarukku avvappōtu utavavum vāyppuk kiṭikkum eṉak karutiyē aṅkut taṅkalāṉār.
nelliyil tiru. mā. tiraviyam piḷḷi eṉpavarōṭu tiruvaraṅkarum iṇintu paṅkutārarkaḷic cērttuttakka caṭṭattiṭṭaṅkaḷōṭu 1920il tirunelvēlit teṉṉintiya civacittānta nūṟpatippukkaḻakam limiṭeṭ eṉṟa niṟuvaṉattit toṭaṅkiṉārkaḷ. iruvarum iṇintē ip paṇiyic ceytārkaḷeṉṟālum tiruvaraṅkar muyaṟciyum uḻippumē itil mēlōṅki niṉṟaṉa. nelliyilēyē taṅkik kaḻakappaṇiyi iṭiyaṟātu ceytu koṇṭiruntār tiruvaraṅkar.
ivarukku illattuṇiviyāy vāyttavar maṟimali aṭikaḷāriṉ tirumakaḷār nīlāmpiki ammiyār āvar. niṟinta tamiḻppulami vāynta ivvammiyār tam tantiyārukkē taṉittamiḻ eḻucciyit tōṟṟuvittā
reṉṟāl ivaratu taṉittamiḻ ārvatti evvāṟu pukaḻvatu! aṭikaḷār taṉittamiḻ iyakkam kaṇṭu ataṉvaḻi nūlkaḷ pala pārttu veḷikkoṇarntatil ivvammiyārukku mikunta paṅkuṇṭu. ammiyār eḻuti veḷivantuḷḷa taṉittamiḻ nūlkaḷum, vaṭacoṟṟamiḻ akarātiyum tamiḻukkuk kiṭitta viramaṇip pēḻikaḷ! ivvammiyār ālōcaṉikaḷum tiruvaraṅkarukku mikunta payaṉ aḷittirukka vēṇṭum!
tiruvaraṅkar oru taṉimaṉitarāka niṉṟu tam tiṟaṉālum muyaṟciyālum āṟṟiya paṇikaḷ namakku viyappit tarukiṉṟaṉa. ippaṇikaḷiliruntu tamiḻmoḻi mītum, civattiṉ mītum ivarukkirunta āḻamāṉa paṟṟiṉi uṇarntu koḷḷalām. teṉṉintiya civacittānta caṅkam, tirukkuṟaḷ tērvukaḷ, tirukkuṟṟālattil cāral mānāṭu, nellit tiruneṟit toṇṭar kuḻām, teṉṉintiya tamiḻccaṅkam, meykaṇṭa cāttira mānāṭu, icittamiḻkkaḻakam pōṉṟa ettaṉiyō amippukaḷum nikaḻccikaḷum ivaratu īṭu iṇiyaṟṟa tiṟamiyi veḷippaṭuttum.
tam iḷiyavariṉ tiṟaṉiyum ceytoḻil nērttiyiyum ciṟanta kuṟikkōḷ teḷiviyum naṉkaṟintavar tiruvaraṅkar. ceṉṉiyil kaḻakattiṉ kiḷiyamippu oṉṟit toṭaṅki ap poṟuppit tam iḷiyavariṭamē (tiru. va.cu. piḷḷiyiṭam) oppuvittār.
kaḻakam toṭaṅkiya kālattiliruntu aḵtu āṇṭukku āṇṭu mikunta nūlkaḷi veḷiyiṭṭut tam vaḷarcciyiṉik kāṭṭiyatu. toṭakkakālak kaḻaka veḷiyīṭukaḷē kaṇkavar vaṉappuṭiyaṉavāy viḷaṅkiṉa. mēlināṭṭiliruntu veḷivantu koṇṭirunta nūlkaḷukkoppat tamiḻil accut teḷivōṭum, aḻakiya kaṭṭukkōppōṭum, nāṭṭukkum moḻikkum nalam tarum ciṟanta nūlkaḷik kaḻakam veḷiyiṭṭu vantatu. taram tāḻnta nūlkaḷiyō, tam kuṟikkōḷukku māṟupaṭṭa nūlkaḷiyō kaḻakam orupōtum veḷiyiṭṭa tilli. ivvamippu nūṉmuṟi, aṉṟumutal iṉṟuvari aṉivarālum pārāṭṭappaṭṭu varukiṟatu. kaḻakap patippu eṉṟāl atu ciṟanta patippu eṉṟa poruḷit tarumpaṭiyāka nūlkaḷ amintu viḷaṅkiṉa. atuvē kaḻakac ciṟappukkum kāraṇamāyiṟṟu.
ettaṉiyō eḻuttāḷarkaḷ, pulavarkaḷ, kaviñarkaḷ ākiyavarkaḷit tamiḻmoḻikku aṟimukam ceytu vitta perumi, tiruvaraṅkarukkuṇṭu. puttam putiya nūlkaḷ, paḻiya nūlkaḷukkup putturikaḷ, kaṟṟōrēyaṉṟi maṟṟōrum kaṟṟup payaṉ aṭiyattakka eḷiya urinaṭip patippukaḷ, ciṟuvar nūlkaḷ pōṉṟavi veḷivarat tam tampiyārukku ivarē uṟutuṇiyāka niṉṟu vaḻikāṭṭalāṉār.
1934l ceṉṉi mākāṇat tamiḻar mānāṭu naṭattavum, ceṉṉi mākāṇat tamiḻar caṅkam amikkavum pēraṟiñarkaḷōṭu tiruvaraṅkarum, avar iḷiyavar tiru. va.cu. piḷḷiyum iṇintu ceyalāṟṟiya paṇikaḷ kuṟippiṭat
takkavi. tamiḻiṉ tūymiyiyum, iṉimiyiyum pēṇu vataṟkum, piṟamoḻit tākkattiliruntu tamiḻik kāppataṟkum, tamiḻp pātukāppuc caṅkam amittu ataṉvaḻi aṟiñar perumakkaḷōṭu kaḻakattiṉ inta iraṭṭiyarkaḷum cērntu ceyta paṇikaḷ maṟakkaṟpālaṉavalla. kaḻaka itaḻākiya centamiḻc celviyit tamiḻ vaḷarccip paṇikkākavē payaṉpaṭutti vantati yāvarum aṟivar. ippaṭi ettaṉi ettaṉiyō paṇikaḷ. kaḻakap paṇiyiyē tam muḻunērap paṇiyākakkoṇṭu kaḻakattik kaṭṭākak kāttup pēṇivanta tiruvaraṅkaṉārukkuttāṉ ettaṉi cōtaṉikaḷ! avaruṭaṉ kaḻaka amiccarkaḷāka iruntavarkaḷē avarukku etirāka māṟi avar mītu vēṇṭāta paḻiyic cumattiṉārkaḷeṉṟāl itaṉiṉum tuṉpam tara vēṟu eṉṉa vēṇṭum? tammītu cumattiya kuṟṟaṅkaḷ aṉittum tavaṟāṉavi eṉpatip paṅkāḷikaḷ kūṭṭattil takka cāṉṟukaḷōṭu ivar meyp pittuk kāṭṭiṉār. piṉpu aṉivarum ivaratu nērmiyi naṉku terintukoṇṭaṉar. attakiya ceyal muṟiyi aṉṟu ivar mēṟkoḷḷāviṭṭāl iṉṟu kaḻakam eṉṟa ōr amippē iruntirukkātu. aṉṟiliruntu tiruvaraṅkar oruvarē paṅkāḷikaḷil nampikkikkuriya amiccarāy amarntu kaḻakappaṇiyāṟṟi varalāṉār. nīṇṭakāla naṇparkaḷum tiruvaraṅkari naṉku aṟintavarkaḷum avarukku māṟupaṭṭu muraṇpaṭṭa ceyalkaḷil īṭupaṭṭapōtu, avar neñcam kaṉṟippōṉatil viyappētum illiyē!
kuṭumpattilum tuyaram tarum pala nikaḻccikaḷ toṭarntu naṭipeṟṟaṉa. ōyvē illāta uḻippum, mīṇṭum mīṇṭum vanta mōtiya tollikaḷum, tuyaraṅkaḷum tiruvaraṅkar uṭal nalattiṟku ūṟu viḷittaṉa. avartam uyiriyē (1944il) kavarntaṉa. ām, avar iṉṟu illi. āṉāl avar ūṉṟiya vitikaḷ muḷittu vaḷarntu pala payaṉmaraṅkaḷ koṇṭa poḻilākik kaṉikaḷi utirkkiṉṟaṉa. ik kaṉikaḷ tamiḻ moḻiyiṉ ciṟappākavum, tamiḻar vāḻviṉ nalamākavum, civattiṉ uyarvākavum kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. kālamellām ik kaṉikaḷ utirntukoṇṭē irukkum.
vāḻvellām tamiḻ vaḷarccikkum, tamiḻar nalaṉukkum ayarātu uḻitta oru māmētiyiṉ vāḻkki varalāṟṟi viṟuviṟuppum cuviyum koṇṭa iṉiya tamiḻnaṭiyil eḻutiyuḷḷār pulavar irā. iḷaṅkumaraṉār. tamiḻ vaḷartta cemmaliṉ varalāṟu muḻumiyum tamiḻmaṇam kamaḻkiṟatu. nūlip paṭikkat toṭaṅkiyatum paṭittu muṭikkum vari uḷḷam vēṟu etilum cellāta nilikku, iḵtu īrppuc cakti koṇṭu viḷaṅkukiṟatu. pala nikaḻccikaḷi nam kaṇmuṉ naṭappaṉapōl ōviyappaṭuttik kāṭṭum āciriyar tiṟaṉ pārāṭṭutaṟkuriyatu.
iv varalāṟṟip paṭikkum vāyppup peṟṟōr iv varalāṟṟu nāyakarippōl tāmum āka vēṇṭum eṉa avāk koḷḷutal iyalpu. nalluḷḷamum nayakkum kuṟikkōḷum koṇṭavarkaḷ potumip paṇiyilaṉṟō ciṟappik kāṇpar. ac ciṟappiyaṉṟō vāḻkkiyil tām peṟum uṇmiyāṉa veṟṟi eṉṟum koḷvar? av veṟṟiyiṉi viḷakkik kāṭṭum oru ciṟanta nūlē itu.


76, āriṅṭaṉ cāli,		
ceṉṉi - 31.
(om) pā. naṭarācaṉ
6-6-82


FOREWORD
K. RAMALINGANAR, M.A.

The life history of Thiru. V. Tiruvarangam Pilli, entitled ‘Arangar’ is a valuable addition to the very few biographical works in Tamil. Tiruvarangam, shortened as ‘Arangar’, in this book, impressed me as a man of action, when I used to meet him now and then at Madras, before 1938. The love and reverance I had for the venerable Marimali Adigal and the fact that Tiruvarangam Pilli became the beloved Son-in-law of that great savant endeared Arangar to me.
In 1938, I was posted to Palayamcotti as Municipal Commissioner and Tiruvarangam Pilli became a close acquaitance of mine. The acquaitance grew into friendship and gradually into fraternity. We used to meet each other frequently. During my visits to his house, his spouse Neelambigi Tiruvarangam and his aged mother Sundaram were hospitable to me.
Tiruvarangam Pilli made his appearance outside dressed in a ‘dhoti’ with coat on and an upper garment over the latter. The coat had huge pockets bulging with all sorts of useful scripts and documents collected from knowledgeable persons and scholars of merit, whom he met while on his literary pursuits. I had never seen the pockets empty. When once he visited me in my house, I showed him the notebook in which I had copied my poems. He read them with avidity and expressed a desire to take the notebook with him so that the poems could be printed and published.
From my personal contacts with him and the impressions I gined therefrom, I can vouchsafe that a true and graphical account of his personality has been portrayed in this biography. Tiruvarangam Pilli’s passion for work cannot be better expressed effectively than what has been rendered by the biographer in the book under review. Irrespective of the venue of his activity-Tuticorin or Colombo, Madras or Tirunelveli-and avocation-clerk or partner of a company or an orginator and promoter of the South India Saiva Siddhanta Works Publishing Soceity, he deployed the same fervor.
The diction employed by the author in this biography is so attractive and the style classical, that I could brook no interruption, while absorbed in reading it. The language is chaste and rythmic and at the sametime strong and powerful depicting how Tiruvarangam Pilli met the arguments of those who opposed him, baffling them.
Arangar, as Tiruvarangam Pilli is affectionately called in this book, was undunted, while not only those who differed from him generally, but also those, supposed to be close to him, picked holes in the performance of his duties relating to the Saiva Siddhanta Works Publishing Soceity. He was sure of his ground. There was nothing wrong or out of the way in what he did, but the defects attributed to his work were either baseless or invented by the jealous of him or due to faulty understanding and misinformation. He stood like a rock aginst odds and but for him, the South India Saiva Siddhanta Works Publishing Soceity would not have grown to such dimensional proportion, as it is today.
Arangar held strong views on the question of speaking and writing chaste Tamil, avoiding induction of words from Sanskrit and other languages into Tamil. He felt perfectly convinced that the Tamil language not only lost its natural form and charm by such an admixture, but also that such dilution of the language had given room for the false notion that Tamil was derived from Sanskrit. The dilution had paved the way for Kannada, Telugu, Malayalam and Tulu to break away from it as separate entities. Had not Sanskrit words forged their way into Tamil, these languages would not have sprung up and the South would be speaking one language today. He was not worried by the bogey of those, dubbing persons advocating the use of chaste Tamil as ‘Puritanic.’ Himself a firm believer in the use of pure Tamil and an advocate of the same, his devotion to the venerable Marimali Adigal and his marital relationship as the consort of his dughter Tirumathi Neelambigi Ammiyar, made him more stunch in this respect.
One of the noteworthy achievements to his credit was the shaping of his younger brother Subbiah Pilli in his mould, the future Managing Director of the Publishing Soceity, which owes its superb and enviable position in the book publishing field to him. Arangar, a hard Taskmaster given a rigid trining to his brother in the ways and methods of developing this publishing house. Subbiah Pilli, in turn, proved worthy of the confidence and brought name and fame to the Soceity by his strenuous efforts. He made it hum with activity and stands as monument to the memory of his brother ‘Arangar’.
The biographer deserves to be feliciated for the masterly way in which he has mobilised almost all the important facts of Arangar’s life and marshalled them in logical sequence, using forceful and inimitable language with flourish and fragrance. Even those evincing not much interest in the book as a biography, would get fascinated to read and re-read it for sheer enjoyment and the wonderful way in which the biographer had woven his fabric in beautiful prose.



nūlāciriyar nutalvu
				kaḻaka ilakkiyac cemmal
			pulavar irā. iḷaṅkumaraṉ			
				talimit tamiḻāciriyar
				mu. mu. u. paḷḷi, maturi
kaḻaka āṭciyar tāmaric celvar va.cu. avarkaḷiṉ maṇiviḻā 1957 il nikaḻntatu. appoḻutu kaḻakap pulavar tirumiku pa. irāmanāta piḷḷi avarkaḷ, kaḻaka āṭciyar avarkaḷiṉ varalāṟṟuc curukkam varintār. piṉṉar va.cu. avarkaḷiṉ 75 ām āṇṭi niṟivu kuṟitta pavaḷa viḻā 1973 il nikaḻntatu. appoḻutu veḷiyiṭappeṟṟa pavaḷaviḻā malaril avarkaḷ varalāṟṟic caṟṟē virittu yāṉ eḻutiṉēṉ. piṉṉark, kaḻaka maṇiviḻā 1982 caṉavariyil nikaḻntatu. atu pōḻtu av varalāṟṟi mēlum yāṉ viritteḻutiṉēṉ. atu, 350 pakka aḷavil, arumi vāynta paṭaṅkaḷuṭaṉ veḷippaṭṭatu. ākaliṉ, va.cu. avarkaḷiṉ varalāṟu toki, vaki, viri eṉṉum nūl vaki mūṉṟiyum oruṅkē koḷva tāy amintatu. viriyiṉ viriyum vēṇṭum eṉṉum viḻumiya viḻivuṭiya perumakkaḷum uḷar eṉpati niṉikka iṉimi mēlum eḻukiṉṟatu.
va.cu. avarkaḷiṉ varalāṟu veḷiyīṭṭukku aṇiyañ ceyyuṅkāl avarkaḷ uḷḷattil ōr uṟuttutal eḻuntatu. tamiyēṉ varalāṟṟu nūlik kaḻaka maṇiviḻāvil veḷiyiṭuvatu paṟṟi eṉ niṉivukku nēṟṟu vantapōtu eṉ arumit tamiyaṉār avarkaḷiṉ ciṟappu mikka varalāṟṟu nūliyaṉṟō mutaṟkaṇ eḻutac ceytu veḷiyiṭutal vēṇṭum; piṉṉarē eṉatu varalāṟṟu nūli veḷiyiṭuvatu poruttamāyirukkum eṉṟa eṇṇam eṉ maṉat takattē tōṉṟi varuttukiṉṟatu eṉṟu varunti eḻutiṉar (8-12-81).
atukāl eṉ iṭakkaṇṇiṉ karumaṇiyi oṭṭi vaḷarntirunta veṇpaṭalat taciyi aṟuvi maruttuvam ceytiruntēṉ. ataṉāl, taṅkaḷukku aṟuvi maruttuvam ceyyap peṟṟa kaṇ cevvitāka vēṇṭumē! eṉ ceyvatu! eṉ maṉattil ēṟpaṭṭa uṇarcciyi uṇmi aṉpuḷḷa taṅkaṭkut terivikkak kaṭamippaṭṭuḷḷēṉ. ippoḻutu eḻuta muṭiyāviṭiṉum eṉatu karuttit taṅkaḷukkut terivikkak kaṭamippaṭṭuḷḷēṉ. ataṟkut tāṅkaḷ kavali koḷḷa vēṇṭā eṉṟu kuṟittaṉar.
16-12-81 il eṉ arumi aṇṇā avarkaḷ kaḻakam toṭaṅkiyatu mutal avarkaḷ vāḻnta kālam variyuḷḷa kuṟippukkaḷ celvi toṭaṅkiyatu mutal 1944 vari veḷivanta cilampukaḷil kāṇalām. meykaṇṭa cāttira mānāṭṭi 7 nāḷkaḷ 1941 il naṭattiṉārkaḷ. atuvē avarkaḷ uṭalukkup  pērūṟāka ēṟpaṭṭatu. tirukkuṟṟālac cāral mānāṭu mūṉṟō nāṉkō naṭattiṉār. nelliyil civap periyar mānāṭu ciṟappāka naṭipeṟṟatōṭu, teṉṉintiya cittānta caṅkattiṉ cārpil civa mānāṭukaḷ naṭipeṟṟaṉa eṉṟum iṉṉavāṟu pala kuṟippukaḷic cuṭṭiṉar.
perumakaṉār va.cu. avarkaḷiṉ ārvattiyum kaṭap
pāṭṭuṇarviyum eṇṇi makiḻnta yāṉ tiruvaraṅkar varalāṟṟuk kuṟippukaḷit tokukkalāṉēṉ. iv varalāṟṟukkuk kiṭitta karuvikaḷ palappala. avaṟṟuḷ kuṟippiṭattakkavi ēḻu. avi :
1.	centamiḻc celviyiṉ mutal irupattoru 
	cilampukaḷ.
2.	pērā. tirumiku maṟi. tirunāvukkaracu avarkaḷ 
	tiruvaraṅkarum nīlāmpikiyārum iyaṟki eytiya 
	kāliyil varinta varalāṟṟuc curakka nūlkaḷum, 
	tavattiru. maṟimaliyaṭikaḷār varalāṟṟup peru 
	nūlum.
3.	peruntiruvāṭṭi cuntarat tammiyār pirivu kuṟittuc 
	centamiḻc celvi cilampu 27 il itaḻāciriyar 
	varinta varalāṟṟuk kuṟippukaḷ.
4.	maṟimaliyaṭikaḷ tiruvaraṅkarukku varinta
	kaṭitaṅkaḷum, tiruvaraṅkarum nīlāmpikiyārum va. cu. 
	avarkaḷukkum piṟarkkum varinta kaṭitaṅkaḷum.
5.	kaḻakam veḷiyiṭṭa palvēṟu malarkaḷilum tamiḻc 
	cāṉṟōrkaḷ varinta kuṟippukaḷ.
6.	va.cu. avarkaḷ avvappōtu varintuḷḷa kuṟippu 
	kaḷum, nērilum viḻākkaḷilum nekkuruki niṉṟu 
	uritta urikaḷum.
7.	kaḻakam veḷiyiṭṭa palvēṟu aṟikkikaḷum ceytik 
	kuṟippukaḷum.
ik karuvikaḷiyum piṟavaṟṟiyum vaḻaṅkiya perumakkaḷukku iv varalāṟṟu nūl āciriyaṉ eṉṉum niliyil naṉṟi kūṟum kaṭappāṭuṭiyēṉ.
iv varalāṟṟuk kippaṭi niliyil paṭittum paṭikkak kēṭṭum urukiya perumakkaḷ iruvar. avarkaḷ va. cu. avar kaḷum maṟi. tirunāvukkaracu avarkaḷum āvar. araṅkaroṭu muṉṉavarum ampikiyāroṭu piṉṉavarum uṭaṉ piṟappurimi uṭiyar allarō! ākaliṉ ivarkaḷ uṭaṉ piṟantu uṭaṉ vaḷarntu, nīruṭaṉāṭic cīruṭaṉ peruki ōluṭaṉāṭṭap pāluṭaṉuṇṭu, palluṭaṉeḻuntu colluṭaṉ kaṟṟup paḻamiyum payiṟṟiyum paṇpum naṇpum viḻuppamum oḻukkamum māṭciyum uṭiyavar eṉṟu iṟiyaṉār kaḷaviyal urikārar niral peṟavurikkum urittiṟa uṇmiyi meyppittu eṉṉiyum urukkiṉar.
amiccar araṅkar vaḷḷuvar vakutta amiccar tiṟaṅkaḷellām oruṅkē amintavar. amiccarukku vaḷḷuvar vakukkum tiṟaṅkaḷ irupatām. avi : ceyalukkuriya karuvi ceytaṟkām kālam, ceyyum vaki, ceyyum aruñceyal añcāmi, naṟkuṭip piṟappu, kākkum tiṟaṉ, kaṟṟaṟinta aṟivu, ayarā muyaṟci, vēṇṭārip pirittal, vēṇṭuvāri iṇittal, pirintu vantārip piṇittal, āyntu terital, tērntu ceytal, oru taliyākat tuṇital, aṟaṉ aṟital, āṉṟaminta collal, tiṟaṅkaṇṭaṟital, nūlaṟivu, nuṇṇaṟivu eṉpaṉa ivvirupatum tiruvaraṅkarukkup peruvaraṅkaḷāka vāyttavi eṉpati iv varalāṟṟik kaṟpār eḷitil aṟintu koḷvar.
oru varalāṟu curuṅkiyatā? virintatā? ivaṟṟik karutuvatu mutaṉmiyaṉṟām. varalāṟṟiṉ nōkkam niṟi vēṟiyuḷḷatā eṉpatē mutaṉmiyāṉa nōkkamām. annōkkam in nūlil niṟivuṟuttap peṟṟuḷatu eṉṟē nampukiṉṟēṉ.
ēṟattāḻa irupattintu āṇṭukaḷāka eṉ vāḻvi muṟṟilum tamiḻ vāḻvāka ākki, eḷḷattaṉip poḻutum payaṉiṉṟi oḻiyāmal iṉiya tamiḻp paṇi ceytaṟkut tiruvaruḷ tuṇiyāl vāytta peruntaki tāmaric celvar 
va. cuppiyā piḷḷi avarkaḷ āvar. avarkaḷukku in nūṟpaṇi nōkkiyum naṉṟi kūṟuṅ kaṭappāṭuṭiyēṉ.

celvam, tirunakar
maturi - 6.
tamiḻt toṇṭaṉ, 
irā. iḷaṅkumaraṉ.
26-4-82



1. pukaḻoṭu tōṉṟiya kaḻakam
tōṉṟiṟ pukaḻoṭu tōṉṟuka eṉpatu poyyā moḻi. maṉitat tōṟṟattiṟkurittāy aminta im moḻi, ōr amippiṉ tōṟṟattiṟkum urimi pūṇṭatēyām pukaḻoṭu tōṉṟiya pulami mintar pērārvattāl pukaḻoṭu tōṉṟiya pulamit tiruviṉar tuṇiyāl tōṉṟiyatu teṉṉintiya civacittānta nūṟpatippuk kaḻakam ākum.
kaḻakattiṟku itupōḻtu (1980) maṇiviḻā. atu tōṉṟiya āṇṭu 1920. aṟupāṉ āṇṭukaḷiṉ muṉṉē tōṉṟiya kaḻakam āṟṟiyuḷḷa paṇikaḷ poṉṉeḻuttukaḷil poṟikkat takka perumi uṭiyatām. ataṉi āytaṟku oru neṟi muṟiyi vakuttut tiṟaṉuṟat tantuḷḷaṉar, tērccimikka tiruvāḷarkaḷ. am muṟi etirmaṟi muṟiyām!
tiru. piḷḷiyarkaḷik kāṇumpōtellām, niṉikkum pōtellām avarkaḷatu acikkalākāta aṉṉi moḻip paṟṟum taṇikkalākāta taṉittamiḻ ārvamum eṉ neñcatti īrttu iṉpuṟuttum. tamiḻttāy ceyta tavappayaṉāy avarkaḷ tamiyaṉār tiru. va. tiruvaraṅkam piḷḷiyavarkaḷum tiru. piḷḷiyavarkaḷum aimpatu āṇṭukaṭku muṉpu, tirunelvēlit teṉṉintiya civacittānta nūṟpatippuk kaḻakattit tōṟṟuvittirāmal iruntāl, tōṟṟuvittum ōyātu muṉintu iṉṟuḷḷa arumperu nilikkuk kaḻakam uyarumāṟu uḻittirāmal iruntāl, ittaṉi ilakkiya ilakkaṇa aṟivu cāl nūlkaḷi ivvaḷavu tiruttamāṉa muṟiyil ittuṇi aḻakaḻakāṉa vaṭivaṅkaḷil tamiḻulakam eṅṅaṉam peṟṟup payaṉaṭiya iyaṉṟirukkum eṉa kallūri, kumarakuruparar kuḻintippaḷḷi, nīlāmpikiyār paltuṟip payiṟcip paḷḷi, kā.cu. piḷḷi ārāycci maṉṟam iṉṉapala aṟappaṇi amippukaḷi niṟuvip pēṇi vaḷarttalāl, it tamiḻ kūṟu nallulakam peṟṟuvarum nalaṅkaḷic collavum kūṭumō?
cīriya kūṭṭuṟavu niṟuvaṉattiṟku eṭuttukkāṭṭā, cevviya itaḻukku eṭuttukkāṭṭā, kuṟivilā niṟivām patippukku eṭuttukkāṭṭā, puttakak kaṭṭaṭa eḻilukku eṭuttukkāṭṭā, vīṭṭu nūlaka vippukku eṭuttukkāṭṭā, tokupput tiṟaṉukku eṭuttukkāṭṭā - kaḻakam eṉṉum orē viṭitāṉē ivaṟṟukku uṇṭu!
taṉittamiḻ maliyām maṟimaliyār toṇṭu viḷakkamuṟa, moḻiñāyiṟu pāvāṇar tam centamiḻc coṟpiṟap pākkappaṇi tulakkamuṟa, pēraṟiñar mu va viṉ tirukkuṟaḷuri vīṭellām oḷiceyya vāytta paṇikkaḷari kaḻakamē yaṉṟō!
ik kaḻakam tōṉṟiṟ ṟilliyēl eṅkum tamiḻ etilum tamiḻ eṉṉum muḻakkam eḻuntirukkumō? ulakat tamiḻp palkalik kaḻakamum, ulakat tamiḻc caṅkamum, ulakat tamiḻ ārāycci niṟuvaṉamum iṉṉapiṟa pēramippu kaḷum tōṉṟutaṟku vaḻivakuttut tanta vaḷamaṉi kaḻakam eṉpati eṇṇātirukka iyalumā?
kaḻakan tōṉṟiyatāl tōṉṟiya payaṉkaḷ iviyeṉiṉ, ak kaḻakattiṉ niṟuvaṉar ataṉ amiccar, kaḻakan tōṉṟiya nāḷ toṭṭu innāḷvari ataṉ ovvoru ceyal tiṟattilum oṉṟi uṭaṉāki iyakkivarum kaḻaka āṭciyāḷar tāmaric celvar va. cuppiyā piḷḷiyiṉ uṭaṉpiṟappāy, vaḻikāṭṭiyāy, kuruvāy amintavar ākiya tiruvaraṅkaṉār tōṉṟiya payaṉē mūlamum, muḷivum viḷivumāy amintaṉa eṉpatu veḷippaṭi. ākaliṉ avartam varalāṟṟi aṟital, tamiḻaka maṟumalarcci varalāṟṟi aṟitalām eṉṉum kuṟippuṭaṉ aṟivōm.


2. teyvat tiruvaṭivam
kaṟcili eṉat takunta kaṭṭuṭal; caṟṟu vaḻukkiyuṭaṉ kūṭiya kaṉatta periya tali; nīṟuviḷaṅkum paranta neṟṟi; periya kaṇkaḷ; iṉikka iṉikkap pēcum kuṟunaki mukam; akaṉṟa mārpu, veḷḷiya uṭcaṭṭi; caṭṭippikaḷ yāvaṟṟilum pala vakiyāṉa kaṭitak kaṭṭukaḷuṭiya pottāṉ pōṭāta mēṟcaṭṭi; iṭuppiṟ cuṟṟiyuḷḷa cempaṭṭu; kaṇukkālukku mēṟpaṭṭa veḷḷi vēṭṭi; aintaṭi uyaramuḷḷa caṟṟuk kuṟinta uyaram; nimirntu etirnōkki naṭakkum vīranaṭi - ivi, tamiḻt tiruvaraṅkariṉ tōṟṟameṉa avartam mittuṉar maṟi tirunāvukkaracu kaṇṭatu kaṇṭavāṟu urikkum kaviṉuri.
tōṟṟattāl ciṟantu viḷaṅkiya tiruvaraṅkar, tamakkut tamiyaṉārāka amintu vaḻikāṭṭiya ēṟṟattit, tāmaric celvar va. cu. tam pavaḷa viḻāvil niṉivu kūrntār :
eṉṉi inta nilikku āḷākkiviṭṭavar eṉ tamiyaṉār avarkaḷē. avarkaḷ paḻiya itaḻkaḷit tokuttuk koṇṭē iruppārkaḷ. paḻiya nūlkaḷit tēṭit tokuttuk koṇṭē iruppārkaḷ. appaṭiyellām avarkaḷ eṉakku vaḻikāṭṭiyatip piṉpaṟṟittāṉ nāṉ appaṭiyē ceytuvarukiṟēṉ. maṟimaliyaṭikaḷ toṭarpiṉālē intat taṉittamiḻt toṇṭu ceyyum vāyppu eṉakku ēṟpaṭṭatu. ātalāl, eṉakku kiṭikkappeṟum pukaḻukkum - perumikkum uriyavarkaḷ eṉ arumit tamiyaṉār tiruvaraṅkaṉār avarkaḷum, taṉittamiḻt tanti maṟimaliyaṭi kaḷārumē āvar (centamiḻc celvi cilampu 47, pakkam 744)
eṉpatu tam tamiyaṉāri niṉivukūrnta tampiyār uri.
intiya varalāṟu teṉṉakattil toṭaṅkiyē eḻutap peṟavēṇṭum eṉpatu ārāycciyāḷarkaḷiṉ munti muṭivu. iṉṟi aṟiñarkaḷō, ulaka varalāṟē teṉṉakattil iruntu toṭaṅkiyē eḻutappeṟa vēṇṭum eṉpati valiyuṟuttukiṉṟaṉar. ulaka mutal tāymoḻi, tamiḻē eṉpatiyum, māntaṉ mutaṟkaṇ tōṉṟiya iṭam, kumarik kaṇṭamē eṉpatiyum teḷivupaṭuttiyuḷḷaṉar. ittaku pukaḻvāyntatu teṉṉakam.
teṉṉakattilum teṟkē cellac cellat tamiḻiṉ cīrmi ciṟantu viḷaṅkutal kaṇkūṭu. iṉṟum paḻantamiḻ vaḻakkāṟukaḷ paḻutupaṭṭuviṭāmal pōṟṟik kākkum vaḷamiyōṭu ilaṅkuvatu paḻampukaḻp pāṇṭināṭēyām. pāṇṭi nāṭṭiṉ ciṟappāka, mupporuḷkaḷ ōtappeṟu kiṉṟaṉa. avi muttu, teṉṟal, tamiḻ eṉpaṉa.
tiruviḷiyāṭal purāṇam pāṭiya parañcōtimuṉivar iṟivaṉ teṟku nōkki naṭampurivatu ēṉ? eṉa oru viṉāvi eḻuppik koṇṭu, viṭiyiṟukkiṟār: iṟivaṉ taṉ āṭalāl viḷiyum iḷippit teṉṟaṟ kāṟṟu taṉ mukattil viciṟutalāl nīkkikkoḷvataṟkum, tirucceviyāl teṉṉaṉ tamiḻamiḻtam parukik kaḷiyāṟutaṟkum teṟkunōkki naṭampurikiṉṟāṉām! ittaku teṉṉāṭṭiṉ naṉṉāṭām tirunelvēli cārnta pāḷiyaṅkōṭṭiyil piṟantār tiruvaraṅkaṉār.
pāḷiyaṅkōṭṭiyil muttucāmippiḷḷi eṉpār pērum perumiyum viḷaṅkat tikaḻntār. avartam arumit tirumakaṉār vayiramuttuppiḷḷi. peṟṟōr tam makkaḷik kaṇṇē maṇiyē vayiramē muttē eṉṟu pārāṭṭuvatu vaḻakkam. pāḷi muttō, tam makaṉi vayiramuttākap peyariṭṭuc cīrōṭum ciṟappōṭum vaḷarttār. akkāla muṟippaṭi uriya vayatil toṭakkak kalvi kaṟpittut tam maraput toḻilil payiṟṟiṉār. inniliyil uriya tirumaṇa paruvam cārntār vayiramuttu.
pāṭu pukaḻ peṟṟa koṟkip paḻanakaril aiyaṉpiḷḷi eṉpār oruvar iruntār. avartam arumit tuṇiviyār pēcciyammiyār. ivarkaḷ celvak kuḻantiyāka 1868il ōr arumi makavu piṟantatu. am makaviṉ puṟa aḻakum aka aḻakum karuti vittāṟ pōlac cuntarattammi eṉap peyarcūṭṭi vaḷarttaṉar! peyar viḷaṅkavanta avvaḻakammi, aḻakuruvām maṇapparuvam cārntār. avarē vayiramuttāṉ aḻakukku aḻaku ceyyum vāḻkkit tuṇiyāka amintār. vayira muttoṭu aḻakucārnta arumi, iyaṟkiyoṭu poruntiya iṉimi vāyntatēyām. avvāṇṭu 1884. 
vayiramuttu cuntarattammi iṇinta iṉiya vāḻvu ettakiyatu! poṉṉum maṇiyum - muttum - pavaḷamum - vayiramum oṉṟupaṭṭu viḷaṅkum aṇimāli taṇiyā aḻakiṉataṉṟō! avvaḻaku ceytatu ivarkaḷ vāḻvu! kuṟiñcip pūvil tēṉi eṭuttuc cantaṉa marattil cērttu vikkappeṟṟa tēṉiṉ cuvikkum naṟumaṇattiṟkum oppuṇṭō? appaṭi oppaṟat tikaḻntatu ivarkaḷ vāḻvu! ivarkaḷ iṉiya ilvāḻviṉ payaṉākak kiḷarnta makkaḷ aivar. avarkaḷ muttucāmi, tiruvaraṅkam, aiyamperumāḷ, cuppiyā, kuppammāḷ eṉpār. tiruvaraṅkar 1890ām āṇṭu mēttiṅkaḷil piṟantār.
kātal iruvar karuttu orumittu naṭāttiya illaṟam, patiṉintu āṇṭukaḷkūṭa muṟṟa muṭiya naṭantēṟiṟṟilli. ataṟkuḷ 1899il vayiramuttar iṟiyaṭi eytiṉār. ciṉṉañ ciṟu makkaḷik koṇṭa muppatu vayatē nirampiya cuntarattammi eṉṉa pāṭupaṭṭiruppār! pañcupaṭāp pāṭupaṭṭu, makkaḷi nōkki maṉavuṟutiyāl vāḻnta avar, tantiyumākit tam kaṭaṉ āṟṟiṉār. tāymiyiṉ perumiyē perumi! veṟukkattakka vāḻvilē, urukkam talippaṭṭu viṭukiṟatu! kuṭumpap perukkamē kuṟiyāyp, peṇmi peruvāḻvu vāḻac cūḻkiṉṟatu! itu paṭippiṉ arumippāṭēyām!
iḷamaṉivikkum iḷamakkaḷukkum tantiyār vippu niti vittuc ceṉṟārā? nilapulaṉkaḷ nirampa viṭṭuc ceṉṟārā? kaṉṟukālikaḷ kaṇakkiṟantu iruntaṉavā? illi! illi; vaṟumiyoṉṟē vippup poruḷāy viṭṭuc ceṉṟār! vaḻi vaḻi vanta vīṭu oṉṟumē uṭimiyāy iruntatu!
toṇṭāṟ paḻutta tamiḻk kiḻavar cuppiyā piḷḷi avarkaḷ, tam tantiyārippaṟṟit tam aṉṉiyār vaḻiyāka aṟinta ceytikaḷi niṉivu kūrntu cuṭṭiyuḷḷaṉar :
eṉ arumit tantiyār avarkaḷ tiruvuruvam eṉ maṉak kaṇṇil teḷivākap paṭavilli. avarkaḷ tōṟṟam perumpāṉmi eṉ arumi aṇṇaṉār va. tiruvaraṅkaṉār pōla irukkumām. tirumaṇam ākumpōtu avarkaṭku 27 akavi irukkum eṉat terikiṉṟatu. appōtu eṉ arumi aṉṉiyārkku akavi 16. avarkaḷ kaṭṭuttiṭṭamāṉa uṭal amippuṭiyavarkaḷ eṉṟum yāvariṭamum aṉpāyp pēci naṭpuk koḷḷum taṉmi uṭiyavarkaḷ eṉṟum, uṟaviṉar kaḷiṭamum aṉpu kāṭṭi varupavarkaḷ eṉṟum avarkaḷ viḻi kavarcci taruvatāy irukkum eṉṟum aṉṉiyār terivippa tuṇṭu.
palvakip poruḷkaḷ viṟkum kaṭi (ṣāp)yil kaṇakku vēlipārttu vantārkaḷām. tirunelvēlikkuc ceṉṟu pa.ta, eṉṟa mukavariyiluḷḷa tulukkar kaṭiyil poruḷkaḷi vāṅkuvataṟkuc celvārkaḷām. appaṭip pōy varukiyil orunāḷ aiyā, vayiramuttupiḷḷi! nīr contamāka oru kaṭi toṭaṅkum. umakku vēṇṭiya poruḷ kaḷik kaṭaṉākat tarukiṟēṉ eṉṟu collavē taṉikkaṭi tiṟantu naṭatti vantārkaḷām. appaṭi naṭatti varumpōtu mottamāka āmaṇakku muttu, maṇṇeṇ ṇeyt takarat tūkku (ṭiṉ) ākiyavi vāṅki vīṭṭil vittum, viyāḻakkiḻami tōṟum cantiyil vittum viṟka ēṟpāṭu ceytārkaḷām. inta muṟiyil toḻil vaḷarntōṅki varukiyil tiṭumeṉak kāyccalum neñcu valiyum ēṟpaṭṭu mūṉṟu nāḷ vari tuṉpuṟṟu iṟiyaṭi cērntārkaḷām. appōtu eṉ mūtta tamiyaṉār muttucāmi piḷḷikkuk kaṭiyi naṭattuvataṟkup paḻakkam pōtātu. ciṟu piḷḷit taṉam. uṟaviṉar evarum utavi ceyya muṉvaravilli. eṉavē, mūṉṟu āṇṭukaḷil kaṭiyi niṟutti atiluḷḷa miccap poruḷkaḷi uṟaviṉar maḷikik kaṭi oṉṟil vittuviṭṭu antak kaṭiyil paṇi ēṟṟukkoṇṭār.
koṭitu koṭitu vaṟumi koṭitu
	ataṉiṉum koṭitu iḷamiyil vaṟumi
eṉṟār auviyār. ataṉiṉum koṭitu, iḷaṅkim miyāyk, kuṟukuṟu naṭantu ciṟuki nīṭṭum iḷamakkaḷip pēṇik kākkum koṭu vaṟumiyām!
ak koṭu vaṟumiyiṉ iṭiyē aṉṟō, āliṉ aṭimaram cāyavum vīḻtu marattit tāṅkik kāppatu pōlac cuntarattammiyār kuṭumpattik kāttār! avar kātta kāval kuṭumpak kāval oṉṟu maṭṭumā? avaṉ aṉṟu kātta kāvaliṉ ūṭṭamum tiṟamum uḻippum allavō, aruntamiḻ aṉṉikkup paṉṉūṟu aṇikalaṅkaḷip pūṭṭip pūṭṭip pukaḻuṟuttum naṉmakkaḷ iruvarit tantatu! anta vayiramaṇic celvaṅkaḷi vayiṟṟakattut tāṅki vaḷarttut toṇṭukku āḷākkit, toṇṭar cīr paravuvār pōlat tam makkaḷ toṇṭilē tiḷittu niṉṟa tūya teyvat tāymi vāḻvu atuvēyaṉṟō! ākaliṉ, iṭumpikku iṭumpi tantu, iṉmikku iṉmi ceytu iṇiyaṟṟu vāḻnta avvarumi aṉṉiyār vāḻvu uṉṉuntōṟum urukkamum atē poḻutil uramum ūṭṭuvatāyt tikaḻkiṉṟatām!


3. vāḻum vaḻikāṭṭi
kuṭumpa nili eppaṭi? muppattōrāṇṭuk kimmi nōṉput tāy! patiṉmūṉṟāṇṭu muttucāmi! pattāṇṭut tiruvaraṅkar! ēḻāṇṭu aiyam perumāḷ! mūṉṟāṇṭuc cuppiyā! ōrāṇṭum nirampāta kuppammāḷ! varuvāy? varu vāy iruntāltāṉē varuvāy uṇṭu? poruḷ varum vāyē aṭipaṭṭuviṭṭatē!
muttucāmi tantiyār vittucceṉṟa kaṭiyi mūṭi uṟaviṉar kaṭiyil paṇiyil amarntār! tiruvaraṅkar kalvik kātal taṭiyuṟṟatu. paṭippi iraṇṭām paṭivattuṭaṉ muṭittu mūṉṟām paṭivam cellum aḷavilē paṭippi viṭuttu vēli tēṭiṉār. anta iḷam paruvam vēlik kuriyatō? kalvikkuriya vaḷamiyāṉa iḷami, vaṟumiyāl vēlikkut taḷḷuṇṭu ceṉṟatu. vaḻakkaṟiñar oruvariṉ eḻuttarāka ēviṟṟu. av ēvutal viruppantaravilli. virivil ataṉi viṭuttu vēṟu vēlikku kiḷappiṟṟu! vēli kikkuḷḷēyō kiṭikkiṟatu? pāḷiyaṅkōṭṭiyi viṭuttu tūttukkuṭikkuc ceṉṟār iḷiya araṅkaṉār. tuṭippum tuṇivum kūṭiviṭumāṉāl iḷamikku īṭu uṇṭō? ciṟṟāḷ vēli eṭṭāḷ vēli eṉṉum paḻamoḻi meyyeṉpati aṟiyār evar?
kālpōṉa pōkkilē pōykkoṇṭiruntālum ākūḻ oṉṟu amintuviṭumpōtu kiyil kaṉap poruḷ cikkiviṭum pōlum! kuṟumpūḻ vēṭṭuvaṉ vaṟuṅkiyum varumē; yāṉi vēṭṭuvaṉ yāṉiyum peṟumē eṉṉum kōpperuñcōḻaṉ poruṇmoḻik kāñci (puṟam. 214) meyyātaṟkup pōlum, araṅkaṉār vēli tēṭit tūttukkuṭikkuc ceṉṟami!
tūttukkuṭiyil vaḻutūr aḻakiya cuntaram piḷḷi eṉpār oruvar iruntār. avar civaneṟic celvar; cemporuḷ tuṇiviṉar; tūttukkuṭi civacittānta capiyiṉ toṭarpu uṭiyavar. āciriyar maṟimaliyaṭikaḷ
māṭṭup pēraṉpu kaṉinta peruntiruvāḷar. avartam uṟavum uḻuvalaṉpum iḷiya araṅkarkku vāyttatu, vāṉpār payirkku vaḷamaḻi poḻintatu pōlāyiṟṟu! tikkut teriyāta kāṭṭil terumantu alamaruvārkkut tici terintu vaḻikāṭṭum tiṟalāḷi uṟavu vāyttami pōl āyiṟṟu! ammaṭṭō, vāḻat tuṭikkum vaḻitēṭuvārkku vaḻikūṭṭum vaḷḷaṉmiyum vāyppatupōl āyiṟṟu! tūttukkuṭiyil nērnta tiruppamē araṅkaṉārit tamiḻ araṅkarāyc ceytatu eṉpatu veḷippaṭi.
tūttukkuṭi vaṇika niliyamoṉṟil paṇi ceytār tiruvaraṅkar. vaṇikap payiṟci peṟutaṟku vāyppum, varuṅkāla vāḻvukku aṭittaḷamumākat tūttukkuṭi vāḻvu amintatē aṉṟi, maṉaniṟivāṉa paṇiyākavō, tam kuṭumpattiṉ muṭṭaṟukka utavum varuvāyāṉa paṇiyākavō vaṇika niliyap paṇi amiyavilli. in niliyil āṅkiruttal kuṭi ceyval eṉṉum oruvaṟku ākāmiyi uṇarntār araṅkar. vīṭṭār icivu kēṭṭuc celvatum iyalātu eṉa uṇarntār. ataṉāl vaḻutūr aḻakiya cuntaraṉār vaḻikāṭṭaliṉpaṭi, tūttukkuṭiyil iruntu kappal ēṟik koḻumpukkuc ceṉṟār.
koḻumpu cērnta piṉṉarē tam arumi aṉṉiyārkkum, uṭaṉpiṟantārkkum ceyti viṭuttār. araṅkariṉ pirivu kuṭumpatti alittatu. eṉiṉum avartam ārva muyaṟcip perukkum urukkiyatu! kuṭumpap poṟiyum, ataṉ nalamum karuti irupālum niṟivu koḷḷum nilimi uruvākiyatu. itu nikaḻntatu 1907ām āṇṭilākum. appoḻutu araṅkaṉār akavi 17.
koḻumpu mānakar cērnta araṅkar, tirunelvēliyic cārnta * cicuva. eṉṉum vaṇika niṟuvaṉattil paṇi ēṟṟār. paṇi eṉṟāl eppaṭi? kaṭikkaṇakku eḻutum eḻuttar paṇi! eḻuttāḷar eḻuttukaḷi ellām muttiriyiṭap piṟanta perumakaṉār, kaṭikkakku eḻutum paṇiyil īṭupaṭṭār. appaṇikku, aṇi cērkka amittār. ūḻiyum vellum uram; uyarnta kuṟikkōḷ; kuṭumpattik kākkum puravāṇmi; tammic cāṉṟōrākkum takavu; tammic cārntārāl talimiyākak koḷḷappeṟum pīṭu; tamiḻk kalvi ūṟṟam; camayac cālpum cikkeṉap piṭikkum kaṭippiṭiyum; neñcārnta nērmi; vañcamaṟṟa uḻippu; ulakattōṭu oṭṭa oḻukum oppuravu; tēṉeṉa iṉiya kūṟit tiḷikkum nānayam; kālan tavaṟāk karutukōḷ; cuṟṟan taḻūum urimippāṭu; talimiyi matittoḻukum taṉipperun takavu; putiyaṉa kāṇṭalil peru vēṭki; aṟivārāycciyil elliyillā ārvap perukku-ivvaḷavu arum peruṅkuṇa nalaṅkaḷum iḷamiyilēyē vāynta peruntiruviṉar eppaṇiyil pukuntāl eṉṉa? enta iṭattil entac cūḻalil iruntāl eṉṉa? kālamum iṭamum cūḻalum kikaṭṭi niṉṟu maṭi taṟṟut tuṇi ceytalukku aiyamuṇṭō?
tiruvaraṅkaṉār īḻattil irunta kāliyil uḻitta uḻippi uṭaṉiruntu kaṇṭavar, piṉṉāḷil maturit tiruñāṉa campantar tirumaṭat talivarākat tikaḻnta 
tiru. cōmacuntara ñāṉacampanta paramāccāriyār avarkaḷāvar. avarkaḷ araṅkarip paṟṟiya niṉiviṉi oru muṟikku irumuṟi niṉivu kūrntu eḻutiṉār :
nam pēraṉpar tiruvaraṅkampiḷḷi avarkaḷ, eṭutta kāriyatti muṟṟuppeṟac ceyvatil caliyā uḻippum, taḷarā ūkkamum, ayarā ārvamum, añcā neñcamum, tamiḻppaṟṟum iṟivaṉiṭattil māṟāta aṉpum koṇṭavarkaḷ. avarkaḷ nāḷ oṉṟukku mūṉṟu maṇi nēram maṭṭum tūṅki, pākki 21 maṇi nēramum uḻittu vanta kālamum uṇṭu eṉṟu kaḻakattil 1008vatu veḷiyīṭṭu viḻā malarukku eḻutiya vāḻtturiyil terivikkiṟār. ataṉ mēlum viḷakkamākak kaḻakap poṉ viḻā vāḻttil terivittuḷḷār maturi aṭikaḷār.
ik kaḻakattiṟkup paṅkukaḷ cērttu variyaṟukkappeṟṟa niṟuvaṉamāka uruvākka ataṟkeṉṟē avataritta namatu matippiṟkuriya tiruvāḷar va. tiruvaraṅkam piḷḷi avarkaḷ evvaḷavu pāṭupāṭṭārkaḷ eṉpati nām naṉkaṟivōm. avarkaḷ ilaṅkiyil irunta kālattil iravu mūṉṟu maṇi nēram maṭṭumē tūṅkuvatum maṟṟa 21 maṇi nēramum ayarātu uḻittu vantatum nām nēril kaṇṭu viyappuṟṟirukkiṟōm. aḷavukku miñciṉa uḻippāl piṉṉar avarkaḷ uṭal ciṟitu nalivuṟṟatum uṇṭu. āṉāl,
ceyaṟkariya ceyvār periyar ciṟiyar,
	ceyaṟkariya ceykalā tār
eṉṟa tirukkuṟaḷiyum,
tuṉpam uṟavaṉiṉum ceyka tuṇivāṟṟi,
	iṉpam payakkum viṉi
eṉṟa tirukkuṟaḷiyum avarkaḷ naṭimuṟiyil kiyāṇṭu vantataṉ kāraṇattiṉālētāṉ intak kaḻakatti veṟṟi
karamāka avarkaḷ naṭattivara muṭintatu.
tiruvaraṅkariṉ vaḷamāṉa iḷaneñcil aḻakiya cuntaram piḷḷi, maṟimali eṉṉum vittiṉi ūṉṟiṉār allarō! avvittu muḷittu iliyoṭu veḷippaṭum vāyppum aṭuttē nikaḻntatu. aṭikaḷār eḻutiya nūlkaḷit tiruvaraṅkar taruvittār. avaṟṟip parukuvaṉ aṉṉa ārvattarāyp payiṉṟār; payiṉṟa ceytikaḷi vāḷā oḻiya viṭāmal tammottavarkkum, uyarntavarkkum payiṟṟiṉār. iyalpilēyē, kātalākik kacintu kaṇṇīr malki ōtuvār tammi naṉṉeṟik kuyppatu, vētam nāṉkiṉum meyp poruḷāvatu, nātaṉ nāmam namacciyāyavē eṉṟu kaṇṭavar araṅkar. ataṉi aṭivataṟkuk kurumūrttamāy eḻuntaruḷi iruppavar tavattiru maṟimaliyaṭikaḷārē eṉṉum teḷivukku vantār! ātalāl, tam neñcat tāmari iruppil aṭikaḷāri irutti vittu vaḻipaṭṭār. kāṇāmal kaṇṭaṭinta kurumaṇiyik kaṇṇuṟak kaṇṭukaḷippuṟum kātal perukiṟṟu. itaṉip perukkiyatil mīṇṭum tūttuk kuṭikkum, aḻakiya cuntaraṉārkkum perum paṅku uṇṭu.
1910ām āṇṭu mutal tavattiru maṟimaliyaṭikaḷār cila āṇṭukaḷ toṭarntu tūttukkuṭi civacittānta capiyiṉ āṇṭu viḻāt talimi pūṇṭār; talimikkaṇ, arumperum poḻivukaḷ ceytār; piṟar poḻivu toṭarpākavum niṟivuri yāṟṟiṉār. aṭikaḷār varuki mutal viṭipeṟal īṟāka nikaḻnta nikaḻccikaḷiyum, vaḻaṅkiya amuta moḻi kaḷiyum tūttukkuṭi aṉparkaḷ vaḻiyākavum, kuṟippāka aḻakiya cuntarar vaḻiyākavum araṅkar aṟintār. aṭikkaṭi avaṟṟik kēḷviyuṟṟu, eṉṟu kolō kaṇkuḷirak kāṇu nāḷē eṉṟu ēkkuṟṟār. in niliyil aṭikaḷārik koḻumpukku aḻittup payaṉ koḷḷum oru muṭivukku vantār.
tiruvaraṅkar, uṇmit tamiḻar, uḻuvalaṉpar, urimit toṭarpar, vaṇikap perumakkaḷ, vaḷḷaṉmic celvar, paṭṭam patavi vīṟuṭiya tamiḻ aṟiñar ākiyavarkaḷōṭu aṇukkat toṭarpu koṇṭu aṭikaḷārik koḻumpukku aḻittup pēṟu koḷḷum viḻuppatti eṭutturittār. naṉmakkaḷ kūṭṭam nayantu pārāṭṭiyatu. araṅkaṉār ārvattiṟku araṇākip pulamit tuṇiyum poruḷ tuṇiyum puriyak kiḷarntatu. araṅkaṉār viḻivu veṟṟik kaṉiyāka ilaṅkalāyiṟṟu.
pallāvarattil iruntu koḻumpuc celavu mēṟkoṇṭa aṭikaḷār, tūttukkuṭi civacittānta capiyiṉ āṇṭu viḻāvil talimiyēṟkum poṟuppum ēṟṟuk koṇṭiruntār. 20-12-1913, 21-12-1913 ākiya iraṇṭu nāḷkaḷum ariya poḻivukaḷ ceytār. 24-12-1913il tūttukkuṭi kīḻūr civañāṉap pirakāca capi viḻāvil, ceṉṉi kiruṭṭiṇavēṇi ammiyār pulamit tiṟaṉip pārāṭṭip paṇṭiti eṉṉum paṭṭam vaḻaṅkic ciṟappittār. 4-1-1914il tūttukkuṭiyil tiruñāṉa campantarip paṟṟip poḻintār. 
6-1-1914 māliyil tūttukkuṭiyil iruntu kappalēṟik koḻumpukkuc ceṉṟār. 7-1-1914 kāliyil koḻumput tuṟiyi aṭintār aṭikaḷ.
* cicuva. eṉpatu cāttāṉ kuḷam civakāminātapiḷḷi, ammaṉpuram cupparāya piḷḷi, kulacēkara paṭṭiṉam vayiravanātapiḷḷi eṉṉum mūvarum naṭāttiya kūṭṭu niṟuvaṉac curukkap peyarākum.



4. īḻattil tamiḻmali
aṭikaḷ vantucērum kappali aṟintu koṇṭirunta araṅkar, tam aṉparkaḷum aṇukkarkaḷum nakarapperu
makkaḷum tiraṇṭirukka varavēṟṟu makiḻntār. ataṟku muṉṉar aṉpuk kaṭitaṅkaḷ vāyilāka maṭṭum aṟintirunta araṅkariṉ iḷamip poliviyum, vaḷamiyuḷattiyum nayattaku paṇpu nalaṅkaḷiyum nēril kaṇṭaṟinta aṭikaḷār maṭṭaṟṟa makiḻvu koṇṭār. tammi īḻattiṟku iv viḷiñartāmō aḻittār? ivartam ārvamum āṟṟalumtām eṉṉē! eṉṉē! iv viḷiñarpāl iṅkuṟiyum poruṭ celvarkaḷum aruṭ celvarkaḷum koṇṭuḷḷa īṭupāṭu
tām eṉṉē! eṉṉē! emakkut toṇṭu ceyya veṉṟē iṟivaṉ iv varaṅka vaḷḷali viṭuttaruḷiṉāṉ kollō! eṉṟu palavāka eṇṇi uḷantatumpiṉār; uvaki kūrntār!
tavattiru aṭikaḷār camayap pulamiyiyum tamiḻ vīṟukōḷiyum nūlkaḷ vaḻiyākavum, avartam poḻivukaḷik kēṭṭuc cuvitta ārvalar vaḻiyākavumē aṟintirunta araṅkar, aṭikaḷāriṉ tōṟṟap polivilum tammi maṟantu mayaṅki oṉṟiṉār! civakkōlam eṉpatum itutāṉā! centaṇmiyum antaṇmi eṉpatum itutāṉā! eṉṉi vaḻivaḻiyāka āṭkoṇṭaruḷa vēṇṭumeṉṟa pēruḷḷap perukkākiya tiruvaruṭ kōlam eṉpatum itutāṉō! vaṇaṅki vaḻipaṭattakka iṟi mūrttam eḷiyēṉ poruṭṭāka oruvaṭivu koṇṭu eḻuntaruḷiyirukkum pēṟē pēṟu! eṉṟu tīrāk kātalarāy uḷḷam aḷḷūṟi ūṟṟeṭukka niṉṟār.
varavēṟpu aṟimukam eṉpaṉa vellām muṟiyē nikaḻntaṉa. aṭikaḷārkku vaḷamelām oruṅku aminta vaḷamaṉi oṉṟil taṅkum vāyppuc ceyyap peṟṟiruntatu. mutaṟ kūṭṭam 10-1-14ām nāḷ māliyil koḻumpu tampiyā cattirattil nikaḻntatu. toṭakka nikaḻcci evvāṟu amintatu?
peruṅkūṭṭamākat tiraṇṭatu. uṇmiyāṉa camaya aṉparkaḷ kūṭṭam atu. koḻumpu nakaril perumi poruntiya mutaliyār kilāca piḷḷi, aṭikaḷip paṟṟiya arumi perumikaḷi aviyōrkku eṭuttukkūṟi varavēṟṟār.
poṉmēṉiyil kāviyāṭi oḷirum tōṟṟam, mukkāṭiṭṭa tāmari pōṉṟa tirumukap polivu, iṉimiyē uruveṭuttālaṉiya cāyal, āya eḻil vaṭivām aṭikaḷiṉ tirumukatti nōkkiyapaṭiyē aviyōr aciyāmal viḻitta imi mūṭā yōkiyar āṉār. eṅkum malikkāṭṭiṉ amiti. aṭikaḷ puṉitavāy malarntu, kaṇmūṭik, ki kuvittu maṉamuruka-nīrvāra-kaṭavuḷ vāḻttu, ñāṉacampantar vāḻttukaḷip pāṭi muṭittār.
aṭikaḷiṉ ici iṉimi, iṉimikku iṟivaṉākum. avviṉṉōci iṉpa veḷḷattil paṭinta aviyōr tammiyum ulakiyum uṇarntilar. ituvari kaṇṭum kēṭṭu mirāta cuvittirāta iṉpa niliyaṭintaṉar. aviyōr uṇarcci pēcupavariyum paṟṟikkoḷḷum allavā? pēriṉpa ulakattuk kuyil aṉparkaḷē eṉṟu pēcat toṭaṅkiyatu. avvaḷavutāṉ! aviyōr tēṉuṇṭa vaṇṭeṉa mēlum iṉpa mayakkattil kalimakaḷ viḻāp patumikaḷāyc camintaṉar. ellōruṭiya aṟivum aṭikaḷ aṟivil oṉṟiviṭṭatu. kaṟpaṉi kaṭanta aṭikaḷiṉ coṟpoḻiviṉ patippil tiḷikkalāyiṟṟu.
pēcat toṭaṅkiya tamiḻp perumāṉ- civaperumāṉ - etupaṟṟip pēciyiruppār? karuta vēṇṭiyatilli. tiruñāṉacampantarip paṟṟittāṉ. aṭikaḷ tam āruyiriṉum ciṟanta appiḷḷip perumāṉippaṟṟip pēci muṭittaṉar. mutal veṟṟi. muḻu veṟṟi. veṟṟi, veṟṟi peṟa vēṇṭu
meṉṟu tavaṅkiṭantatu, ataṉ payaṉāy aṭikaḷ pēccil kalantu veṟṟi peṟṟateṉṟē colla vēṇṭum. iṉpamō tuṉpamō colliyaṉṟit tīrātu. aṭikaḷiṉ coṟpoḻi
viṉpattil makiḻntavarkaḷ am makiḻcciyit tammuḷ aṭakkum āṟṟalaṟṟavarāyiṉar. aṭikaḷ pēccu avvaḷavu uyarntatu. mūvulakattum kaṭavuḷiṭattuṅkūṭa avvaḷaviṉpam irukkātu. ākavē, maṟunāḷ aṭikaḷ irukkumiṭatti eṇṇaṟṟa aṉparkaḷ nāḷellām cūḻntukoṇṭaṉar. aṭikaḷum caḷikkavilli. vantavarkaḷiṭam iṉpamuṟap pēciṉār. mēlum avarkaḷ iṉpuṟṟuc ceṉṟaṉar. (maṟimaliyaṭikaḷ varalāṟu 205 - 6)
7-1-1914il koḻumpukku vantu cērntu aṭikaḷ 24-3-1914 vari āṅkiruntār. iv viraṇṭarit tiṅkaḷ kālamum tiruvaraṅkar aṭikaḷukku aṉparāy, aṇukkarāy, aṟivaṟinta ārvalarāy, iv vellāvaṟṟiyum kaṭanta toṇṭarāyt tikaḻntār. ettaṉi iṭaṅkaḷil kūṭṭam; ettaṉi perumakkaḷ toṭarpu; evvaḷavu poruḷ taṇṭal; ellāvaṟṟilum tiruvaraṅkar tūya uḷḷam paṭintu paṭintu toṇṭāṟṟiyatu.
īḻattiṟku ceṉṟa aṭikaḷ paṉṉiru kūṭṭaṅkaḷil poḻivu ceytār; tiruvaruṭ celvattiyum, centamiḻc celvattiyum vāri vāri vaḻaṅkiṉār! vaḻaṅkiya aṭikaḷukkup poruṭ celvam vāri vaḻaṅkum kaṭappāṭṭil muṉintu niṉṟār tiruvaraṅkar. aṭikaḷ pallāvarattil tirumāḷiki oṉṟu eḻupputalil ūṉṟiyiruntār. ataṟku vēṇṭum poruṭkuṟiyāl iṭaruṟṟiruntār. ak kuṟiyit tīrkkattakka vakiyil aṭikaḷārkku araṅkar poruḷīṭṭi utaviṉār. aṭikaḷāriṉ mutaṟ cuṟṟulāvilēyē rū. 1888 kiṭikka vaki ceytār tiruvaraṅkar. ammaṭṭō, aṭikaḷāriṉ aṟivākkap paṇikaḷukku utavum vakiyil tiṅkaḷ tōṟum koḻumpuc celvarkaḷ vaḻiyākat toki kiṭikkavum vaki ceytār. aṭikaḷār nūlkaḷit taruvittu viṟṟut tantum utaviṉār. piṉṉē, aṭikaḷār accakam oṉṟu niṟuvutaṟku muṉintār. ataṟkum araṅkar perumporuḷ tiraṭṭit tantār. aṭikaḷār tam aṟivuk kaṭal (ñāṉacākaram) itaḻil tiruvaraṅkar toṇṭiṉi eḻutip pārāṭṭiṉār :
namatu accuk kūṭattiṟkāka aṉparkaḷiṭam poruḷ tiraṭṭit tara muṉvantu niṉṟu, ceṉṟa oṉṟariyāṇṭukaḷāka iṭiyaṟātu uḻittu utavi ceytuvarum nam aṉpuruvāṉa srīmāṉ va. tiruvaraṅkam piḷḷiyarkaḷiṉ pērupakārac ceykikkut tiruvaḷḷuva nāyaṉār,
ceyyāmaṟ ceyta utavikku viyakamum,
	vāṉakamum āṟṟal aritu
eṉṟaruḷic ceytavaṇṇam evvakiyāṉa kimmāṟutāṉ ceyyak kūṭum? nammarumic centamiḻ moḻiyum civa cintānta ñāṉamum paravumporuṭṭu nāmeṭuttuvarum naṉmuyaṟcikaḷ taḷarāmal naṭipeṟa vēṇṭumeṉṟu eṇṇiya avarkaḷiṉ puṇṇiya niṉivum, an niṉivi nilipeṟac ceyta avarkaḷiṉ ariya periya muyaṟciyum ellām valla iṟivaṉaruḷi iṭiyaṟātu iḻukkavallaṉa. avarkaḷ attiruvaruṭ pēṟṟāl varum ellāc celvaṅkaḷiyum peṟṟu emṉōrkku uṟutuṇiyāy iṉitu vāḻvārkaḷāka eṉṟu vāḻttukiṉṟōm. iṉi srīmāṉ tiruvaraṅkam piḷḷiyiṉ vēṇṭukōṭ kiṇaṅkip puṇṇiyat tiruvāḷarāṉa kulacēkaraṉpaṭṭiṉam srīmāṉ rā. pa. centilāṟumukam piḷḷiyarkaḷ eḻunūṟṟimpatu rūpāvum, tayāḷakuṇap pirapuvāṉa ku. pa. periyanāyakam piḷḷiyavarkaḷ ainnūṟu rūpāvum namatu accukkūṭattiṟkeṉṟu tarumamāka utavi, uṭaṉē uyarnta putiya acciyantiram vāṅkumpaṭi muṉ muyaṟciyuṅ kāṭṭiya arumperun takimi eḻumi eḻu piṟappum maṟakkaṟpālataṉṟu.
accakat tiṟappu viḻā aṟikkiyilum araṅkari aṭikaḷ niṉivu kūrntār :
namakkē contamāka ōr accukkūṭam tiṟakka vēṇṭumeṉṟu pala āṇṭukaḷāka uḷḷattiṟ koṇṭirunta peruviruppatti ellā nalattiṟkum uṟiviṭamāṉa nam āṇṭavaṉ tiruvaruḷum avvaruḷvaḻi niṟpavarāṉa aṉpar srīmāṉ va. tiruvaraṅkam piḷḷiyavarkaḷum niṟivēṟac ceyta pērutaviyiṉāl cālivākaṉacakam 1838 naḷa varuṭam kārttiki mātam 26n tēti (12 - 12 - 1916) putaṉ kiḻami kāliyil ēḻari maṇikkuppiṉ eṭṭu maṇikkuḷḷākap pallāvaram namatu camaraca caṉmārkka niliyattiṉkaṇ namakkē contamāṉa ṭi.em eṉṉum peyar vāynta nam accukkūṭam nilipeṟuttappaṭṭuc civa maṅkaḷak kiriyikaḷōṭu tiṟakkappaṭalāyiṟṟu.
accakat tiṟappu viḻā nikaḻnta aṉṟum, ataṟku utaviyām poruṭṭut, tiruvaraṅkar tām naṉkoṭiyākat taṇṭiya rūpā aṟunūṟi viṭuttiruntār. ittakiya arumiyi uṇarntatāltāṉ, aṭikaṭku utavac civapirāṉāl aṉuppappaṭṭavar tiruvaraṅkam piḷḷiyāvar eṉṟum (maṟimaliyaṭikaḷ varalāṟu 199) tiruvaraṅkar utaviyillā viṭṭāl aṭikaḷ kavaliyiṉṟi iṉpamāy - ciṟappuṭaṉ tammaṟiviṉāl tamiḻakattiṉi uyarttirukka muṭiyumā? eṉṟum (mēṟpaṭi 250) nēril uṇarntavar pārāṭṭi makiḻntaṉar.
toṭarntu aṭikaḷārkku muṭṭuppāṭu iṉṟi utaviya tiruvaraṅkar aṭikaḷāri mīṇṭum koḻumpukku aḻittār. 21-5-1917il koḻumpukkup puṟappaṭṭa aṭikaḷār 28-9-1917il mīṇṭār. ēṟattāḻa nāṉku tiṅkaḷāka aminta ic cuṟṟulāvil aṭikaḷiṉ pulamic celvatti īḻam peṟṟup pēriṉpam tuykkavum, īḻap perumakkaḷiṉ naṉkoṭic celvattāl aṭikaḷār tam tavapperum paṇikaḷi mēlum mēlum ciṟakkac ceyyavum vāyttatu. aṉṟiyum araṅkariṉ arumi iḷaval va. cuppiyā avarkaḷum aṭikaḷāruṭaṉ aṇukkarāy aṉparāyt tikaḻavum vāyttatu.
ivviraṇṭāñ cuṟṟulāvil aṭikaḷārkku vēṇṭum nalaṅkaḷellām cūḻnta pēṇiyatuṭaṉ, naṉkoṭi taṇṭi rū. 1797um tiruvaraṅkar vaḻaṅkiṉār. avartam ārvat toṇṭil muḻutuṟa āḻnta aṭikaḷār oru peruṅ kūṭṭattil nīvir nāyaṉmār aṟupattu mūvari aṟivīr; aṟupattunāṉkām nāyaṉār oruvaruḷār. avarē it tiruvaraṅkar eṉṟu pēraṉpu taḻikkap pārāṭṭi makiḻntār. araṅkar, toṇṭarāy  aṭikaḷār toṇṭarcīr paravuvārāy aminta peṟṟi itu.
aṭikaḷārum araṅkarum aṭikkaṭi kaṭitat toṭarpu koṇṭaṉar. aṟivut toṇṭil talippaṭṭa avarkaḷ uḷḷam ārvattāl untappeṟṟu aṉpup piṇippil oṉṟiyatu. eṭṭā iṟiyōṭum māntari iṇittu vikkum aṉpupperukku evariyē iṇikkātu? irupālum perukiya aṉpiṉ mukiḻppu, teyvat tirukkōlamāy uḷḷelām aḷḷūṟi niṉṟatu.
aṉpuruvāṉa celvac ciruñcīvi tiruvaraṅkam piḷḷi avarkaḷukkuc civaperumāṉ ṟiruvaruḷāl ellā nalaṅkaḷum uṇṭākuka. namatu accuk kūṭattiṟkup poruḷutavi ceyya icinta aṉparkaḷiṭam tāṅkaḷ aṉpu kūrntu tiraṭṭiyaṉupyiya rūpā nūṟum vantatu. piṟarkkeṉa vāḻum peruntakimi vāynta taṅkaḷ pēruḷḷattil eḻuntaruḷi yirukkum nam civaperumāṉ taṅkaḷukku nalvāḻ nāḷiyum kaḷaṅkamaṟṟa tūya utavi niṉivukaḷiyum mēṉmēl aruḷit taṅkaḷi ellā vakiyilum mēmpaṭac ceyvāṉāka. tāṅkaḷ iṉṉuñ ciṟitu viṭātu muyaṉṟāl toki viriviṟṟiraḷum. nam kavalikaḷellām akalum; ulakattiṟ ṟamiḻum civamum nattāmaṇi viḷakkaṅkaḷāyt tikaḻum. taṅkaḷ pukaḻum puṇṇiyamuṅ kuṉṟiṉ mēliṭṭa viḷakkup pōl tikaḻum eṉṟu aṭikaḷār 29-6-1916il araṅkarukkuk kaṭitam varintār. pārāṭṭum naṉṟiyum palapaṭa urittār.
ilaṅkikkuc ceṉṟu iraṇṭām cuṟṟulāvi muṭittuk koṇṭu pallāvaram mīṇṭa aṭikaḷār 6-8-1917il eṉatiru kaṇmaṇiyāy aṉpuruvāy viḷaṅkum celvac cīrañcīvi tiruvaraṅkam piḷḷiyavarkaṭku nam perumāṉ tiruvaruḷāl ellā nalaṅkaḷum uṇṭākuka.
taṅkaḷ pēraṉpiṉ perukkālum nam miṟivaṉ tiruvaruḷ vaḷattālum cukamāka iṅku vantu cērntēṉ. nam perumāṉ tiruvaruṭ pēṟṟiyum civa camaya viḷakkattiyu mēyallāmal piṟitetaṉiyum kimmāṟāka nōkkāmal uṇmiyaṉpōṭu uḻittu varum taṅkaḷ arumperuñ ceyal ulakiṉkaṇ maṟṟeṅkum kāṇṭal aritaritu. taṅkaḷik koṇṭē namatu civañāṉa tarmam iṉitu naṭipeṟac ceytuvarum tiruvaruḷukku namatu vaṇakkam uriyatu eṉṟu ārāviruppār eḻutiṉār.
tiruvaraṅkar toṇṭarākavum puravalarākavum aṟivaṟinta aṉparākavum iruppatuṭaṉ tām peṟṟa aṟivunalatti mēṭip poḻiviṉ vaḻiyē vāri vaḻaṅkum vaḷḷalākavum tikaḻa vēṇṭum eṉa aṭikaḷār eṇṇiṉār; mikamika virumpiṉār; ataṟkut tām utavum muyaṟciyum mēṟkoṇṭār. iraṇṭu mūṉṟu nāṭkaḷukkumuṉ upanyāca viṣayam uṅkaḷukku aṉuppiyiruntēṉ. atu vantucērnta vivaram uṭaṉē teriviyuṅkaḷ. iṅṅaṉamē mātam ovvoṉṟu uṅkaṭku eḻutiyaṉuppuvēṉ. nīṅkaḷ tiruvaruṭṭiṟattāl naṉku upanniyacikkum āṟṟal peṟṟut tamiḻiyum civatti ṉiyum eṅkum viḷaṅkac ceyya vēṇṭum eṉpatutāṉ eṉatu peruṅ kōrikki eṉṟu eḻuti vaḻikāṭṭiṉār. āṉāl araṅkar eṉṟum ceyaṟkaḷa vīrarākat tikaḻntārē yaṉṟic coṟkaḷa vīrarākat tōṉṟiṉār allar. oruvēḷi, collāṇmiyiṉum ceyalāṇmiyē aḻiyātatu eṉṟum, ceyalāṇmiyil nilipeṟuttāta collāṇmiyāl payaṉētum illiyē eṉṟum araṅkar uḷḷam tiṭṭap paṭuttiyirukkak kūṭum! ākaliṉ, araṅkar tam vāḻnāḷ muḻumiyum coṟkaḷattil tammip pukuttikkoṇṭār allar.
palapala coṟpoḻikaḷ naṭakka ēṟpāṭu ceytuvanta araṅkar tām coṟpoḻivu ceytalil nāṭṭañ celutta villi eṉiṉum, kaṭitam eḻututalil pērārvam koṇṭi runtār. kaṭitam eḻututalit taṉip peruṅ kaliyākap pōṟṟiṉār. avartam eḻuttum aṭittal tiruttal iṉṟi muttukkōvi pōl ōr oḻuṅkupaṭa irukkum. tāḷiṉ mēlē kiyi vittu eḻutat toṭaṅkiṉāl oru ciṟu taṭiyum illāmal appaṭiyē tāḷiṉ aṭippakuti vari virintu eḻuti muṭikkum tiṟam araṅkarkku iyalpāy iruntatu. noṭip poḻutum, iṭittaṭiyuṟṟuk kaṭitam kaṇaṅkātu! kaṭitac ceytiyum, iḷamit tuṭippum, mutumit tiṟamum oruṅkē kāṭṭavallatāy amintirukkum. payaṉaṟṟa ceyti oṉṟum avar kaṭitattil iṭam peṟuvatē illi.
iṉiya tampiyār va. cu. viṟku 14-8-1913il eḻutiya kaṭitattil kuṟippiṭukiṟār :
 nī iṉimēl tamiḻ eḻuttukaḷi ovvoru variyiṉ mēlum ilakkaṇa vaḻuviṉṟi fullstop, cama, semicolan, colan mutaliya puḷḷikaḷ pōṭa vēṇṭiya iṭaṅkaḷil pōṭṭum, vevvēṟu viṣayamākayiruntāl vevvēṟu pārāp pōṭṭe ḻutiyum eḻuttukkaḷ ovvoṉṟum muttu muttāyum taṉit taṉiyāyum kūṭṭeḻuttu eḻutāmalum eḻutap paḻaka vēṇṭum.
eḻuttukkaḷippaṟṟik kūṟi neṟippaṭuttum tiruvaraṅkar koḷḷattakka naṉṉeṟikaḷ cilavaṟṟiyum kuṟippiṭṭu valiyuṟuttukiṟār :
nittiyappaṭi tēkāppiyācamum ceytuvara vēṇṭum. uṉ vēlikaḷi Time Table pōṭṭu oḻuṅkāy (Regular) aṇukavum picakāmal naṭantu varuvāyeṉa nampukiṟēṉ. LibraryÆš News paper eṭuttu ati vācittu varavēṇṭum. atuvum viruttiyi uṇṭupaṇṇum.
“Om I am very active strong & healthy Sivayanama” itaṉi enta nāḷum kāliyil 15 nimiṣam maṉatil niṉittu orē cintaṉi ceytu varuvāyāṉāl nalla cukamum, palamum, mikunta ñāpaka caktiyum aṭivāy. atāvatu taṉiyāka ōr aṟiyil iruntu maṉatti ōrmiyōṭu celutti uccāṭaṉam paṇṇik koṇṭu varuka.
tampiyār va. cu.vukku araṅkar itaṉi 14-8-1913il eḻutiṉār. appoḻutu tampiyār akavi 16. araṅkar akavi 23. ēḻāṇṭu mūtta tamiyaṉārākavō araṅkar eḻutukiṉṟār? tanti niliyil iruntu kākkum, talimakaṉā rākavaṉṟō eḻutukiṉṟār! tantiyār maṟiyumpōtu cuṭṭik kūṟiya kuṟippu avar uḷḷattiṉ āḻattuḷ āḻamāyp patintu kiṭantatu! nam peruṅ kuṭumpattit toṭakkamutal nāṉē pātukākkak kūṭiyavaṉ eṉṟu aiyar avarkaḷ iṟakkum pōtu cuṭṭik kāṭṭi aṟivuṟuttiya aṟivuriyic ciramēṟ koṇṭu tāmē kuṭumpak kaṭamikaḷi aḷḷip pōṭṭukkoṇṭu araṅkar ceytārakaliṉ, tam cīriḷamip paruvattilēyē ceḻumutiyarāy aṟivuṟutti vaḻikāṭṭum tiṟam peṟṟār eṉal amiyum.


5. peruntaki vaḷḷal
tiruvaraṅkaṉār uḷḷattil aṭikaḷār kōyil koṇṭa nāḷ mutal, evvev vakiyāl ellām avarkku utavalām eṉṟu tiṭṭamiṭṭukkoṇṭē iruntār. avvāṟu tiṭṭamiṭṭuc ceytavaṟṟuḷ iraṇṭaṉic cuṭṭal cālum. oṉṟu tirucaṅkar kampeṉi eṉṉum peyarāl puttaka niliyam oṉṟu toṭaṅkiyatu; maṟṟoṉṟu aṭikaḷāriṉ āyvukku utavum vakiyil tiṅkaḷ tōṟum oru toki kiṭikkumāṟu vaki ceytatu.
tiruvaraṅkariṉ keḻutaki naṇpar vi. caṅkara nārāyaṇapiḷḷi eṉpār. tam peyariyum curukki iṇittut tirucaṅkar kampeṉi eṉṉum peyarāl 1917il oru puttaka niṟuvaṉattit toṭaṅkiṉār tiruvaraṅkar. ip puttaka niṟuvaṉam potuvāka aṉittup puttakaṅkaḷiyum vāṅki viṟṟu vantatu. eṉṟālum, aṭikaḷār nūlkaḷi ilaṅkiyil peritum parappi aṭikaḷukku utavi ceytaṟkākavē tōṉṟiyatākum. piṉṉar in niṟuvaṉattiṉ vaḻiyākavē centamiḻk kaḷañciyam eṉṉum peyarāl aṭikaḷār varinta tiruvācaka virivuri tiṅkaḷ veḷiyīṭāka veḷip paṭṭatu eṉpatum kuṟippiṭattakkatu.
tiruvaraṅkarum caṅkaranārāyaṇarum iṇintu ceyta icceyal pōlavē, tiruvaraṅkarum avartam pēraṉpar 
rāma. pa. centilāṟumukam avarkaḷum iṇintu ceyta utaviyē maṟṟoṉṟu ākum. aṭikaḷār nūlārāycci ceytal, purāṇaṅkaḷukku uri varital ākiya ākkap paṇikaḷ ceyyumāṟu koḻumpu vaṇikap perumakkaḷiṭam tiṅkaḷvāri naṉkoṭit tiṭṭam oṉṟit toṭaṅki utaviṉār. ataṉpaṭi, 1918 mē vari rū. 1599-8-0 kiṭittatu! ivvāṟu kālattāl utavum pēṟu tiruvaraṅkaṉārkku aṉṟi evarkkē vāyttatu? ataṉip peṟṟu makiḻum pēṟum aṭikaḷārkku aṉṟi evarkkē vāyttatu? ivarkaḷ toṭarpē tiruvaruḷ toṭarpu pōlum!
īḻattu vāḻviyellām aṭikaḷār toṇṭukkē araṅkar āṭpaṭuttiviṭṭārō eṉṉum niṉivu eḻumpalām. āṉāl araṅkar tām pukunta vaṇika niṟuvaṉak kaṭaṉi maṟappārō? tam kuṭumpak kaṭaṉi maṟappārō? tiruvaḷḷuvar aṟattuppāl oṉṟaṉōṭu amiyāmal, poruṭpālum iṉpattuppālum ōtiya cīrmiyi uṇarātavarō tiruvaraṅkar! muppālum pōṟṟi oḻukutalē muṟimi eṉak koṇṭamiyār, aṭikaḷārkku utavutali aṟattup
pālāyt, tām pukuntu ceyyum vaṇikap paṇiyip poruṭpālāyt, tam kuṭumpak kaṭami puritali iṉpattup pālāy mēṟkoṇṭu ovvoṉṟum kuṟivuṟā vaṇṇam niṟivuṟuttiṉār. vāy, pēciṉālum, ki, āṭi acintālum, kaṇ, vaḻik kāṭciyik kaṇṭālum, karuttellām talimēl irukkum taṇṇīrkkuṭattiṉ mēlē irukkak kuṭaṅkoṇṭu nīr cumantu celvārik kaṇṭuḷōm allamō! avvāṟu araṅkar avvak kaṭamikaḷip puritalil karuttākavē iruntār.
tikkaṟṟa niliyil irukkum tam kuṭumpattiṟkut tammāl iyaṉṟa aḷavum muyaṉṟu īṭṭiya poruḷik kālam tavaṟāmal viṭuttu vantār. avar ceyta utaviyē aṉṉiyārkku āṟutalāyiṟṟu! tam arumi makaṉār tam kuṭumpac cumiyit tāṅkit takka vakiyil uyarttiyē tīrvār eṉṉum muḻutuṟu nampikkiyi ūṭṭi avarkku uvaki tantatu. uṭaṉ piṟanta iḷiyāruḷ cuppiyāviṉ paṭippil taṉi akkaṟi kāṭṭiṉār araṅkar. tantiyār maṟivāl, tam kalvi niṉṟuviṭṭatu pōl tam tampiyārkkum ākiviṭak kūṭātē eṉṟu añciṉār. ātalāl avar kalvikkut tām ceyya vēṇṭiya poruḷutavikaḷiyellām taṭṭāmal viṭuttār. iḷaval cuppiyāvukkē, tām kuṭumpattiṟku viṭukkum paṇattiyellām viṭuttār. tiṭṭamiṭṭuc celaviṭal, cikkaṉattip pēṇal, tavaṟātu varavu celavu eḻututal, kaṭami tavaṟāmi eṉpavaṟṟi yellām ciṟṟiḷam paruvattilēyē iḷaval cuppiyā eytutaṟkut tōṉṟāt tuṇiyāy niṉṟu vaḻikāṭṭiṉār.
iḷaval va. cu. pāḷiyil uḷḷa tūya cavēriyār uyarpaḷḷiyil payiṉṟār. 1916ām āṇṭu paḷḷiyiṟutit tērvil veṟṟi peṟṟār. appōtu vāyppāka irunta añcalakap paṇiyil cēra virumpiṉār. āṉāl aṇṇal tiruvaraṅkaṉār pēruḷḷam vēṟāka iruntatu. tampiyik koḻumpukku varumāṟu eḻutiṉār. 1916ām āṇṭu cūlit tiṅkaḷil va. cu. koḻumpukkuc ceṉṟār.
koḻumpil irunta mutaṉilik kallūriyil cērntār va.cu. ilaṇṭaṉ mānakara āciriyar kallūri naṭāttum tērviṉi eḻutuvataṟku ak kallūri payiṟci tantu vantatu. attērvukkuc ceṉṟu veṟṟi peṟṟār. maruttuvak kallūriyil cērtaṟkuriya vāyppu uṇṭu. ātalāl am muyaṟciyil ārvamāy ūṉṟutaṟku araṅkar ēṟpāṭu ceytār. tantiyōṭu kalvipōm eṉpati meyppittuviṭakkūṭātu eṉpatē araṅkariṉ kuṟikkōḷāka iruntatu.
ilaṅkiyiṉ paruvanili va. cu. viṉ uṭalnilikku ēṟṟu varavilli. ātalāl, ōyāta nīrk kōvikku āṭpaṭṭu allaluṟṟār. evvakiyālum ataṉit tīrkka muṭiyāmiyāl, ilaṅkiyil iruntu pāḷiyaṅkōṭṭikkē tirumpiṉār.
pāḷiyil iruntālum payilutalil peru vēṭki yuṭiya va. cu. tam payiṟciyi viṭṭār allar. vīṭṭil iruntu koṇṭē tērvukkuriya pāṭaṅkaḷit toṭarntu payiṉṟu tērvukkuc ceṉṟu, āṅku eḻutum muyaṟci mēṟkoṇṭār. avvāṟē uriya kālattil ceṉṟu tērvu eḻuti at tērvil veṟṟiyum peṟṟār.
ilaṇṭaṉ mānakarat tērvil peṟṟa veṟṟi, maruttuvak kallūriyil cēra ūkkiyatu. āṉāl, am muyaṟci veṟṟi taravilli. eṉiṉum tirunelvēli intuk kallūriyil iṭinili vakuppil cērntu toṭarntār. ivaṟṟuk
kellām tūṇṭalum tuṇiyumāka iruntavarum ākkamum ūkkamum tantavarum araṅkarē eṉpatic colla vēṇṭiya
tilli. ākaliṉ, araṅkar camayappaṇi moḻippaṇikaḷil ūṉṟiyiruntārēṉum, tam kuṭumpattiṟkuc ceyyattakka paṇikaḷil karuttākavē iruntār eṉpatu viḷaṅkum.
araṅkarkku iḷamiyilēyē tokuppu ārvam mikkiruntatu. kiṭittaṟku ariya nūlkaḷit tokuttal, cāṉṟōrkaḷ aṭiyārkaḷ paṭam tokuttal, uyarnta peru makkaḷiṉ kaṭitaṅkaḷ tokuttal iṉṉavāṟāṉa tokuppukaḷil iḷantip paruvam toṭṭē pērārvam koṇṭiruntār. poruḷ muṭṭuppāṭu muṉṉiṉṟu takinta pōtumkūṭa arum poruḷkaḷi vili tantu vāṅkic cērttalil karuttākavē iruntār araṅkar! avar piṉṉāḷil tokuppuk kalit tōṉṟalāyt tikaḻntataṟkum, tokupput tuṟiyil iṇiyaṟṟa tōṉṟalāyt tam tiruttampi cuppiyāvit tikaḻac ceytataṟkum, mūlakkūṟu ilaṅkiyil araṅkar irunta nāḷilēyē toṭaṅkiviṭṭatu eṉpati ivaṇ niṉivu kūrtal takum.



6. kātal arumputal
vaṇika niṟuvaṉap paṇikaḷiṉ iṭiyē tuṇit toḻilāka aṉṟō araṅkar tirucaṅkar kampeṉiyi niṟuvik kaṭaṉāṟṟiṉār. ataṉ ceyaṟpāṭum payaṉ viḷiyum araṅkar uḷḷattil ūṉṟiṉa. at toḻiliyē tam vāḻvut toḻilāka koḷḷutal ciṟakkumeṉak karutiṉār. atē poḻutil tam vāḻvi tāyt tamiḻakattukku māṟṟik koḷvatu nalam eṉṟa muṭivukkum vantār. ataṟku aṭippaṭi aṭikaḷārkku aṇukkamākiyiruntu avar paṇikku ollum vakiyāl ellām utavakkūṭum eṉpatākavē iruntatu. ākaliṉ tirucaṅkar kampeṉiyic ceṉṉiyil niṟuvic ceyalāṟṟutaṟkut tuṇintār. at tuṇiviṟku neñciṉ aṭittaḷattil ōr uṇarvum iruntatu pōlum!
tiruvaraṅkaṉāriṉ keḻutaki naṇpar centil āṟumukam avarkaḷippaṟṟi muṉṉarē aṟivōm. avar orukāl pallāvarattiṟku vantu aṭikaḷārik kaṇṭu aḷavaḷāvic ceṉṟār. ceṉṟavar aṭikaḷāriṉ aṉpu māṇpiyum, avar tam illattār viruntōmpu taliyum, aḻakē vaṭiveṭuttu aṟivāṉ mutirntu viḷaṅkum nīlāmpiki eṉṉum aṭikaḷār tam arumic celviyar taṉip perum cīrmiyiyum, aṭikaḷār am makaḷār māṭṭuk koṇṭuḷḷa taṉip pēraṉpiyum, iṉṉa piṟavaṟṟiyum araṅkar tiruc ceviyil cērttiruntār. araṅkar uḷḷattil nīlāmpiki eṉṉum poṟkoṭi miṉṉaliṭat toṭaṅkiyatu. ‘mo fsh®¡F m‹ò kUfuhŒ mikí« ngW thŒ¡Fnkh! eṉṟu tamakkuḷ viṉāvi, aṭikaḷār kāṭṭi varum pēraṉpup peru veḷḷap perukkil nīntum pēṟu vāytta eḷiyēṉ marukarām pēṟu eytalum kūṭuvatē eṉṟu amintu tāyt tamiḻakam nōkkiṉār.
tiruvaraṅkar tamiḻakattil niliyāyt taṅkum tiṭṭattuṭaṉ vantār. koḻumpil iruntu tūttukkuṭikku vantu pāḷiyaṅ kōṭṭikkuc ceṉṟār. tam arumi aṉṉiyāriyum, uṭaṉ piṟantārkaḷiyum kaṇṭu uvaki kūrntār. ciṉṉāḷkaḷ ceṉṟu, ceṉṉikkup puṟappaṭṭār. tam mēṟkoṇṭa toḻilpaṟṟi aṭikaḷāriṭam kalantu uriyāṭa vēṇṭumē! avar karuttaṟintu niṟuvaṉattit toṭaṅki naṭatta vēṇṭumē! nērē pallāvarattiṟkuc ceṉṟu aṭikaḷāri avartam vaḷamaṉiyil kaṇṭār. kaṇṭa kāṭci eppaṭi? varavēṟpu eppaṭi? uṭaṉiruntu kaṇṭa aṭikaḷār celvar urikkiṟār :
aṭikaḷiṉ nalvāḻviṟku uyar toṇṭiṟku uyirāy niṉṟu utaviya vāṉaṉiya vaḷḷalār tiruvaraṅkam oru nāṭkālip poḻutu (9-11-1918) nāṅkaḷ yāvarum ārā vēṭkiyuṭaṉ etirpārttu iruntapaṭi pallāvaram vantu cērntār. avari varavēṟṟu makiḻvatil eṅkaḷ kuṭumpam appari varavēṟṟa appūtiyaṭikaḷ kuṭumpattiyum miñciviṭṭatu. tiruvaraṅkarik kaṇṭu nāṅkaḷ aṭinta makiḻccikku elliyilli. avarip pōṟṟuvatil oruvark koruvar muntiṉōm. aṭiyārkku aṭiyārāka yārum virumpuvar allarō! eṉ aṉpāṉa peṟṟōrkaḷ avarukku vaki vakiyāṉa viruntūṭṭi makiḻntaṉar. tiruvaraṅkarukku appōtu āṇṭu 28. paranta tirumukamum, tirunīṟṟiṉāl niṟinta paranta neṟṟiyum, akaṉṟa peru viḻikaḷum, uyarntakaṉṟa mārpum, kallaṉiya tōḷum tāḷum koṇṭu kaṭṭaminta kāḷiyāk kaviṉ poḻiyum mēṉiyarāyt tiruvaraṅkar ilaṅkiṉār. (maṟimaliyaṭikaḷ varalāṟu. 324)
nīlāmpikiyārkku appoḻutu akavi patiṉāṟu. iḷamiyiṉ vaḷamum aṟivuṉ polivum koṇṭu eḻilmiku naṅkiyāy viḷaṅkiṉār. nākiyic cārntavar allarō aṭikaḷār? ātalāl nākiyil kōyil koṇṭa ammiyiṉ tiruppeyarit tam makaḷukkuc cūṭṭiṉār. nīlāmpikiyār ñu6-8-1903il piṟantār.
nīlā toṭakkap paḷḷip paṭippi ceṉṉiyilum pallāvarattilum payiṉṟār. pallāvarattil ataṟku mēṟpaṭṭa vakuppup paḷḷi ak kālattil illāmiyāl avar paḷḷip paṭippu avvaḷavil niṉṟatu!
paṭippu, paḷḷiyil maṭṭum payilap peṟuvatō? ataṟku iṭamētu? kālamētu? ārvam iruntāl eṅkum eṉṟum kaṟka vāyppu uṇṭē! ulakattip pōlat tiṟantaveḷip paḷḷik kūṭam oṉṟu uṇṭō? atilum aṭikaḷārtam aṉpuc celviyiṉ kalvikkup paḷḷikkūṭam viṭuttami oru taṭiyāmō? mummoḻi valla aṭikaḷār pulami nalam ellām nīlāviṉiṭattut taṅkiyatu. avar tāmum mummoḻip pulamiyum, ilakkiya ilakkaṇat tērcciyum, tirumuṟi tirumuṟit tiṟamum oruṅkē vāykkap peṟṟār. aṭikaḷār, tam makkaḷuḷ nīlāmpikiyārkkē ārvattoṭum kaṟpittār eṉpatum, palvēṟu paṇikaḷukku iṭiyēyum kālamum - kaḷamum vakuttu muṟiyē kaṟpittār eṉpatum karutattak
kaṉavām.
aṭikaḷiṉ kuralō tēṉiṉum iṉiyatām! nīlāmpikiyiṉ kuralō ataṉiṉum iṉiyatām! yāḻum, kuḻalum, kuyilum nīlāmpikiyiṉ kuralukkut tiṟi celuttal vēṇṭumām! aṭikaḷ tōṭi, piravi, āṉanta piravi, mōkaṉam, mattiyamāvati, caṅkarāparaṇam, nīlāmpari mutalāya icikaḷi nīlāmpikikkuk kaṟpittārām!
1916ām āṇṭu! nīlāvukku akavi 13! aṭikaḷār vaḷḷalār pāṭiya,
